# Source conflicts

> Generated by `uv run python -m gcg_sim.sources.conflicts` from the cached snapshots, the
> comprehensive rules, `src/gcg_sim/data/curated_conflicts.json` and
> `src/gcg_sim/data/overrides.json`. Do not edit by hand: edit the curated list or the
> overrides and regenerate. `uv run python -m gcg_sim.sources.conflicts --check` fails when
> this file or `data/conflicts.json` is stale, when a conflict has no valid resolution, or when
> a curated entry's quoted evidence no longer matches its source.

## How conflicts are resolved

The simulator never edits cached source data. Every conflict below has exactly one resolution
in `overrides.json`, naming a policy, the decision and its rationale. Resolutions may pin a
`canonical_product_id` (the printing whose gameplay fields every printing of that card number
uses) and `field_overrides` (field → value, applied to every printing of the listed
`card_numbers` when the card database loads). Order of authority, highest first:

1. **Official errata** — applied from the gcg-api snapshot, or through an `errata_supersedes_print` resolution's `field_overrides` when gcg-api lags the official notice (the `errata-unapplied:*` entries below); verified each run.
2. **Official card rulings and rules-FAQ answers** — they override literal card text and fill
   gaps in the comprehensive rules; the engine implements the ruled behaviour and a test tagged
   `@pytest.mark.ruling` / `@pytest.mark.faq` pins it.
3. **Card text** over the comprehensive rules (rule 1-3-1), using the canonical printing's
   wording: when printings differ, the base printing on the official card list (which carries
   Bandai's current wording) wins; Edition Beta and promotional printings with older wording
   or stats are superseded. Reminder text in parentheses never changes behaviour (rule 2-11-4).
4. **Comprehensive rules**, reading obvious typos and wrong cross-references as intended.
5. **Most defensible reading** for genuinely ambiguous text, explained in the resolution.

Edition Beta cards' tournament legality is governed by the official tournament rules, not by
this report; here they only matter as divergent printings of a card number.

Re-running after a data refresh: new conflicts appear without a resolution and `--check`
fails until a reviewer adds one; a card ruling or rules-FAQ entry that is new, or whose answer
changed, appears as `unreviewed:*` until it is cited by a curated entry or recorded (with its
fingerprint) under `reviewed_without_conflict` in the curated file; curated entries whose
quotes disappear from the sources are flagged stale and must be re-reviewed; resolutions and
review records that no longer match anything are listed as orphaned so they can be deleted.

## Policies

| Policy | Summary | Rationale |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `banlist_most_defensible` | Ambiguities in the official banned/restricted list use the reading recorded by the official-sources review. | The list's wording leaves these points open; the chosen reading is the conservative one for tournament-style validation and is reported in validation messages. |
| `canonical_printing` | When printings of one card number differ, every printing uses the gameplay fields of one canonical printing. | Cards with the same card number are the same card (2-1-1). The base printing on the official card list carries Bandai's current wording: in this snapshot every printing whose gameplay wording differs from it is an Edition Beta, event/promotional, bonus-pack or same-set parallel print, while every later reprint (SC01 Deck Build Box, GD05 reprints, 2026 prize prints) matches it up to reminder text and line order. A later reworded reprint therefore supersedes the older wording. |
| `edition_beta_superseded` | Edition Beta printings whose stats or text deviate are superseded by the standard printing. | Edition Beta was a pre-release product; its deviating records are superseded rewordings or data errors. Whether Edition Beta cards are tournament-legal is decided by the official tournament rules (see data/official), not here. |
| `engine_derives_markers` | keyword_effects/timing_markers are search aids; the engine derives keywords and timings from the effect text. | gcg-api extracts them with a fixed vocabulary that drops 【Pilot】, <Development>, Pilot qualifications and the Activate･Main/Action pairing, and lists keywords a card merely mentions. |
| `errata_supersedes_print` | Official errata replace the printed text: gcg-api applies most of them, a field_override applies any the snapshot lacks, and each run re-verifies both. | Errata are Bandai's official corrections. gcg-api usually applies them to every printing; a field_override covers an erratum gcg-api has not shipped yet (errata-unapplied:*), and the detector fails loudly if a refresh loses one. |
| `faq_clarifies_rules` | Official rules-FAQ answers clarify or fill gaps in the comprehensive rules and are followed as written. | The rules FAQ is published with the rules by Bandai; tests tagged @pytest.mark.faq pin each answer. |
| `most_defensible_reading` | Genuinely ambiguous text gets the most defensible reading, with the reasoning recorded in the resolution. | No official source settles the question; the chosen reading follows the closest rules, rulings and FAQ answers so that later official clarification is easy to adopt. |
| `null_stat_is_zero` | A stat the rules require but the card data leaves empty is 0. | Every Unit and Base has AP (3-2-5, 3-5-4) and the official list shows no value for these cards; 0 is the only value consistent with a blank and with 1-3-6. |
| `rules_obvious_reading` | Typos and undefined wording in the rules take their obvious meaning, consistent with the rest of the rules. | Another rule or an official FAQ states the same thing unambiguously. |
| `rules_xref_intended_target` | A wrong cross-reference in the rules is read as a reference to the section it obviously means. | Cross-references carry no rules content of their own; the cited title (or topic) identifies the intended section unambiguously. |
| `ruling_overrides_text` | Official card rulings override or complete the literal card text and the comprehensive rules. | Rulings are Bandai's authoritative answers for that card; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards. The engine implements the ruled behaviour and a test tagged @pytest.mark.ruling pins it. |
| `text_format_correction` | Obvious formatting defects in the card text are corrected with field_overrides at load time. | The defect would make a template compiler misread the card (missing keyword brackets, ASCII ':' as cost separator); the correction restores the standard template without changing meaning. |
| `token_stats_from_effect` | A token's stats come from the effect that creates it; token card records are informational. | Effects specify tokens in full, e.g. [Gundam]((White Base Team)･AP3･HP3); the standard token cards match those specs while some Edition Beta token records do not. |

## Divergent printings (43)

### `divergent:EXB-001` — EXB-001 EX Base: printings differ in effect

- severity **info**; origin detected
- Cards: EXB-001
- 3 printings of EXB-001 disagree on effect (reminder_only). The differences are cosmetic or confined to reminder text.
- Suggested canonical printing: `EXB-001_p7`
- Field differences:
  - `effect` (reminder_only):
    - `EXB-001_p5`, `EXB-001_p7`: `"(At the start of the game, place 1 active EX Base as your shield area's base.)"`
    - `EXB-001_p6`: `"(At the start of the game, place 1 active EX Base into your shield area.)"`
- Sources: gcg-api card EXB-001_p5 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=EXB-001_p5; gcg-api card EXB-001_p6 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=EXB-001_p6; gcg-api card EXB-001_p7 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=EXB-001_p7
- **Resolution** (`canonical_printing`): Use EXB-001_p7. Only the reminder text differs; the EX Base is placed active in the base section at the start of the game (6-2-3).
  - Rationale: Reminder text has no game effect (2-11-4).
  - Canonical printing: `EXB-001_p7`

### `divergent:EXR-001` — EXR-001 EX Resource: printings differ in effect

- severity **info**; origin detected
- Cards: EXR-001
- 3 printings of EXR-001 disagree on effect (reminder_only). The differences are cosmetic or confined to reminder text.
- Suggested canonical printing: `EXR-001_p7`
- Field differences:
  - `effect` (reminder_only):
    - `EXR-001_p5`: `"(At the start of the game, the second-turn player places 1 EX Resource into their resource area.)\n(Rest an EX Resource then exile it from the game when paying a cost.)"`
    - `EXR-001_p6`: `"(At the start of the game, the second-turn player places 1 active EX Resource into their resource area.)\n(Rest an EX Resource then remove it from the game when paying a cost.)"`
    - `EXR-001_p7`: `"(At the start of the game, the second-turn player places 1 active EX Resource into their resource area.)\n(Rest an EX Resource then exile it from the game when paying a cost.)"`
- Sources: gcg-api card EXR-001_p5 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=EXR-001_p5; gcg-api card EXR-001_p6 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=EXR-001_p6; gcg-api card EXR-001_p7 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=EXR-001_p7
- **Resolution** (`canonical_printing`): Use EXR-001_p7. Only the reminder text differs ('exile'/'remove', a missing 'active'); 6-2-4 and 5-17-3-2-3 govern the EX Resource.
  - Rationale: Reminder text has no game effect (2-11-4); see rules-internal:remove-exile-terminology.
  - Canonical printing: `EXR-001_p7`

### `divergent:GD01-005` — GD01-005 Unicorn Gundam (Unicorn Mode): printings differ in timing_markers, keywords_text, effect

- severity **minor**; origin detected
- Cards: GD01-005
- 5 printings of GD01-005 disagree on timing_markers, keywords_text, effect (derived, substantive). Printings deviating from GD01-005 in gameplay terms: GD01-005_p1, GD01-005_p2, GD01-005_p3.
- Suggested canonical printing: `GD01-005`
- Field differences:
  - `timing_markers` (derived):
    - `GD01-005`, `GD01-005_p3`, `GD01-005_p4`: `["During Link", "Destroyed"]`
    - `GD01-005_p1`, `GD01-005_p2`: `["During Pair", "Destroyed"]`
  - `keywords_text` (derived):
    - `GD01-005`, `GD01-005_p3`, `GD01-005_p4`: `"during link destroyed"`
    - `GD01-005_p1`, `GD01-005_p2`: `"during pair destroyed"`
  - `effect` (substantive):
    - `GD01-005`, `GD01-005_p4`: `"【During Link】【Destroyed】Return this Unit's paired Pilot to its owner's hand. Then, discard 1."`
    - `GD01-005_p1`, `GD01-005_p2`: `"【During Pair】【Destroyed】If this is a Link Unit, return its paired Pilot to its owner's hand. Then, discard 1."`
    - `GD01-005_p3`: `"【During Link】【Destroyed】If this is a Link Unit, return its paired Pilot to its owner's hand. Then, discard 1."`
- Sources: gcg-api card GD01-005 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-005; gcg-api card GD01-005_p1 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-005_p1; gcg-api card GD01-005_p2 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-005_p2; gcg-api card GD01-005_p3 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-005_p3; gcg-api card GD01-005_p4 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-005_p4
- **Resolution** (`canonical_printing`): Use GD01-005: '【During Link】【Destroyed】Return this Unit's paired Pilot to its owner's hand. Then, discard 1.' The older wordings ('【During Pair】【Destroyed】If this is a Link Unit, return …' on _p1/_p2 and the launch-event hybrid _p3) have the same effect.
  - Rationale: The base printing matches the latest reprint (SC01, GD01-005_p4).
  - Canonical printing: `GD01-005`

### `divergent:GD01-088` — GD01-088 Banagher Links: printings differ in timing_markers, keywords_text, effect

- severity **minor**; origin detected
- Cards: GD01-088
- 6 printings of GD01-088 disagree on timing_markers, keywords_text, effect (derived, substantive). Printings deviating from GD01-088 in gameplay terms: GD01-088_p1, GD01-088_p2 (all Edition Beta).
- Suggested canonical printing: `GD01-088`
- Field differences:
  - `timing_markers` (derived):
    - `GD01-088`, `GD01-088_p3`, `GD01-088_p4`, `GD01-088_p5`: `["Burst", "When Linked"]`
    - `GD01-088_p1`, `GD01-088_p2`: `["Burst", "When Paired"]`
  - `keywords_text` (derived):
    - `GD01-088`, `GD01-088_p3`, `GD01-088_p4`, `GD01-088_p5`: `"burst when linked"`
    - `GD01-088_p1`, `GD01-088_p2`: `"burst when paired"`
  - `effect` (substantive):

- **Resolution** (`token_stats_from_effect`): Use T-006 (AP3/HP1), matching '[Char's Zaku Ⅱ]((Zeon)･AP3･HP1)' on GD01-026 and ST03-016; the Edition Beta record's AP1 is ignored.
  - Rationale: Tokens are created with the stats written in the creating effect.
  - Canonical printing: `T-006`


## Errata (3)

### `errata-unapplied:ST12-001:news-02_193` — ST12-001 Gundam Epyon: official errata (2026-09-04) adds 【Once per Turn】; missing from gcg-api

- severity **major**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: ST12-001
- The official notice https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/news/02_193.html (2026-09-04, cached at data/official_raw/en_news_02_193.html) changes '【During Pair･Lv.5 or Higher Pilot】' to '【During Pair･Lv.5 or Higher Pilot】【Once per Turn】'. The pinned gcg-api snapshot (errata.json and cards.ndjson) still has the printed wording.
- Evidence:
  - card ST12-001: “【During Pair･Lv.5 or Higher Pilot】During your turn, when this Unit destroys an enemy Unit with damage”
- Sources: gcg-api card ST12-001 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=ST12-001 (effect)
- **Resolution** (`errata_supersedes_print`): Apply the official errata: the first ability is 【During Pair･Lv.5 or Higher Pilot】【Once per Turn】.
  - Rationale: Official errata (news 02_193, 2026-09-04) supersede the printed text; the snapshot lags the official site.
  - Override `effect` → `"【During Pair･Lv.5 or Higher Pilot】【Once per Turn】During your turn, when this Unit destroys an enemy Unit with damage, deal 2 damage to all enemy Units with 5 or less AP.\n【Deploy】Choose 1 enemy Base. Deal 5 damage to it."`

- severity **info**; origin detected
- Cards: GD04-067
- Printed effect reads 'from your trash'; official errata (2026-04-10) changes it to "from any player's trash". Verified applied in all 2 printing(s) of the snapshot.
- Field `effect`: `"from your trash"` → `"from any player's trash"`

  - `GD04-067_p1`: ok
- Sources: https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/news/02_157.html; gcg-api card GD04-067 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD04-067; gcg-api card GD04-067_p1 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD04-067_p1
- **Resolution** (`errata_supersedes_print`): Use the errata'd effect ("from any player's trash" instead of the printed 'from your trash'); the snapshot already contains it in every printing.
  - Rationale: Official errata of 2026-04-10 (https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/news/02_157.html).

### `errata:T-013` — T-013 Hy-Gogg: printed trait superseded by errata

## Banned/restricted list ambiguities (6)

### `banlist:banlist-scope` — The Comprehensive Rules (6-1-1-3) allow 4 copies of any card number and never me

- severity **minor**; origin detected
- Cards: —
- The Comprehensive Rules (6-1-1-3) allow 4 copies of any card number and never mention the B&R list. The B&R list applies when 'there are occasions, such as official or sanctioned tournaments, when certain cards will be designated as banned or restricted'.
- Sources: official banned/restricted list
- **Resolution** (`banlist_most_defensible`): Enforce the B&R list by default, because benchmarks model tournament play (the library call validate_deck(deck, banlist=False) checks the Comprehensive Rules only).
  - Rationale: Benchmarks model sanctioned tournament play, where the list applies (official rules page).

### `banlist:effective-date-and-region` — The July 2026 list took effect on 2026-07-24 in the US and 2026-07-25 in Asia. T

- severity **minor**; origin detected
- Cards: —
- The July 2026 list took effect on 2026-07-24 in the US and 2026-07-25 in Asia. The current-list page (01_279) is dated 2026-09-25 and gives no effective date. Its only changes are ST11-008 and ST12-010, added to the vanilla list (ST11/ST12 release 2026-09-25).
- Sources: official banned/restricted list
- **Resolution** (`banlist_most_defensible`): Use the current list with effective_date 2026-09-25. The simulator validates against one list, not by date.
  - Rationale: The simulator validates against a single current list, not by date or region.

### `banlist:pair-sideboard-scope` — Banned and restricted cards explicitly cover deck + sideboard. Banned pairs say 

- severity **minor**; origin detected
- Cards: —
- Banned and restricted cards explicitly cover deck + sideboard. Banned pairs say only 'cannot be used at the same time' / 'in the same deck'.
- Sources: official banned/restricted list
- **Resolution** (`banlist_most_defensible`): Not relevant: the simulator has no sideboard, so apply pairs to the main deck.
  - Rationale: The simulator has no sideboard.

### `banlist:starter-deck-exceptions` — Two exceptions exempt unmodified starter decks: ST02 (3x ST02-016 Corsica Base) 

- severity **minor**; origin detected
- Cards: —
- Two exceptions exempt unmodified starter decks: ST02 (3x ST02-016 Corsica Base) and ST05 (ST05-004 + ST05-009). Neither the official pages nor the pinned gcg-api data contain the starter decklists, so the simulator cannot tell whether a deck is an unmodified starter deck. The current-list page (01_279) does not restate the exceptions, but no notice revokes them.
- Sources: official banned/restricted list
- **Resolution** (`banlist_most_defensible`): Do not apply either exception by default, so such decks fail as a restricted or banned-pair violation. Name the exception in the error message. Support the exception only if an official decklist is added to the data with provenance.
  - Rationale: The exemption depends on an official decklist that the offline data does not contain; failing closed with the exception named is safer than guessing.

### `banlist:vanilla-enumeration-vs-predicate` — The vanilla banned-pair rule is defined both by description and by an enumerated

- severity **minor**; origin detected
- Cards: —
- The vanilla banned-pair rule is defined both by description and by an enumerated list (22 cards today). The July 2026 notice says future matching cards are added 'at the time of their release', so the list can lag a new set.
- Sources: official banned/restricted list
- **Resolution** (`banlist_most_defensible`): Enforce the predicate over card data and also check the enumerated list. Today they are identical (predicate_equals_enumeration). The data refresh should fail loudly if they ever diverge.
  - Rationale: The notice defines the pair by attributes and promises to add future matches, so both the predicate and the enumeration are authoritative; checking both catches either one lagging.

### `banlist:vanilla-without-effects-definition` — 'without effects' is not defined. It could exclude only cards with ability text,

- severity **minor**; origin detected
- Cards: —
- 'without effects' is not defined. It could exclude only cards with ability text, or also cards with keyword effects or link requirements.
- Sources: official banned/restricted list
- **Resolution** (`banlist_most_defensible`): Treat a card as having effects when its effect text is non-empty and not '-' (keywords count). A link requirement alone is not an effect. No Lv.2/cost 1/2 AP/2 HP Unit in the pinned data has effect text, so the choice changes nothing today.
  - Rationale: Keyword effects are effects (5-1-2); a link condition is card information (2-12), not an effect.

- keyword_effects is every <Keyword> mentioned anywhere in the text: conditional grants ('While …, this Unit gains <Blocker>'), grants to other Units, target filters ('Unit with <Blocker>') and reminder text. For these cards at least one listed keyword is not an unconditional printed keyword of the card itself.
- Sources: gcg-api src/normalize.js (extractKeywordEffects/extractTimingMarkers)
- **Resolution** (`engine_derives_markers`): Keywords a Unit has come from its compiled effects (printed keywords, conditional and granted ones); keyword_effects is not used to decide which keywords a card has.

### `markers-vocab:activate-split` — 【Activate･Main】/【Activate･Action】 are recorded as separate 'Activate' + 'Main'/'Action'

- timing_markers cannot distinguish a 【Main】 Command from an 【Activate･Main】 ability on the same card; engines must read the bracket from the effect text.

### `markers:GD02-053:unbracketed-keyword` — GD02-053: keyword Suppression written without <> brackets

- severity **major**; origin detected
- Cards: GD02-053
- The effect text names keyword effect(s) Suppression outside the <...> form, so keyword_effects omits them and a template compiler would miss the keyword.
  - `GD02-053`: `["Suppression"]`
  - `GD02-053_p1`: `["Suppression"]`
- Sources: gcg-api card GD02-053 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD02-053; gcg-api card GD02-053_p1 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD02-053_p1
- **Resolution** (`text_format_correction`): Read '[Suppression]' as the keyword <Suppression>: GD02-053 has <Suppression>.
  - Rationale: It is followed by the standard <Suppression> reminder text; square brackets otherwise name cards or Pilots and no card is named 'Suppression'.
  - Override `effect` → `"<Suppression> (Damage to Shields by an attack is dealt to the first 2 cards simultaneously.)\n【During Link】During your turn, while there are 7 or more cards in your trash, all your other (Vulture) Units get AP+2."`
  - Override `keyword_effects` → `[{"keyword": "Suppression", "value": null}]`
  - Override `keywords_text` → `"suppression during link"`

### `markers:GD03-015:ascii-colon` — GD03-015: activated-effect cost separated by ASCII ':'

- severity **minor**; origin detected
- Cards: GD03-015
- Activated effects separate cost and effect with the full-width '：' (rule 10-1-7-2); this text uses ':' so a parser may treat the whole line as a cost-free effect (rule 10-1-7-5).
  - `GD03-015`: `["【Activate･Main】【Once per Turn】Exile 3 (Titans) cards from your trash: This Unit gains <Breach 4> during this turn."]`
- Sources: gcg-api card GD03-015 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD03-015
- **Resolution** (`text_format_correction`): Treat ':' as the cost separator '：'; the activation cost is 'Exile 3 (Titans) cards from your trash'.
  - Rationale: Every other activated effect uses '：'; the text before the colon has the form of a cost (10-1-7-2, 10-1-7-4).
  - Override `effect` → `"【Activate･Main】【Once per Turn】Exile 3 (Titans) cards from your trash：This Unit gains <Breach 4> during this turn.\n\n(When this Unit's attack destroys an enemy Unit, deal the specified amount of damage to the first card in that opponent's shield area.)"`

## Rules cross-references (11)

### `rules-xref:2-7-1` — Rule 2-7-1 cross-references (See 7. Attacking and Battles); means §8 Attacking and Battles

- severity **minor**; origin detected+curated
- Cards: —
- Rule 2-7-1 says (See 7. Attacking and Battles), but section 7 is Game Progression. The intended section is §8 (Attacking and Battles).
- Reference `"(See 7. Attacking and Battles)"`; suggested target `8` (Attacking and Battles)
- Evidence:
  - rules 2-7-1: “(See 7. Attacking and Battles)”
- Sources: Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §2-7-1
- **Resolution** (`rules_xref_intended_target`): Read the reference in 2-7-1 as §8 (Attacking and Battles).
  - Rationale: §8 is the only section titled 'Attacking and Battles' (or, for untitled references, the only section on the cited topic); the cited number is an off-by-one/renumbering slip.

## Rules internal inconsistencies (5)

### `rules-internal:11-3-1` — 11-3-1 says 'becomes zero less' (missing 'or')

- severity **info**; origin curated
- Cards: —
- 11-3-1 reads “When the HP of a Unit, Base, or Shield becomes zero less after receiving damage…”, while 5-5-2 destroys a card that has damage equal to or greater than its HP.
- Evidence:
  - rules 11-3-1: “When the HP of a Unit, Base, or Shield becomes zero less after receiving damage”
  - rules 5-5-2: “A card that receives damage equal to or greater than its HP is destroyed as a result of rules management.”
- Sources: Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §11-3-1; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §5-5-2
- **Resolution** (`rules_obvious_reading`): Read 11-3-1 as 'becomes zero or less': destroyed when damage ≥ current HP.
  - Rationale: 5-5-2 states the same rule unambiguously.

### `rules-internal:base-ap` — Bases 'have AP' and seemingly deal battle damage, but no Base has AP

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: —
- 3-5-4/3-5-4-1 say Bases have AP used in battle and 13-1-5-2 speaks of battle damage 'from the Unit or Base targeted for attack', yet 8-5-2-4 has only the attacker deal damage to a Base, the attack target is always the player (8-2-1), the EX Base has 0 AP (5-17-3-1-1) and every Base record has no AP (see data-null:BASE:ap).
- Evidence:
  - rules 3-5-4: “Bases have AP and HP.”
  - rules 3-5-4-1: “AP shows a Base’s offensive strength in battle.”
  - rules 13-1-5-2: “battle damage from the Unit or Base targeted for attack is not received”
  - rules 8-5-2-4: “If there is a Base in the enemy shield area, the attacking Unit deals an amount of battle damage equal to its AP to the Base targeted for attack.”
  - rules 5-17-3-1-1: “An EX Base is a Base token with 0 AP and 3 HP.”
- Sources: Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §3-5-4; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §3-5-4-1; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §13-1-5-2; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §8-5-2-4; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §5-17-3-1-1
- **Resolution** (`most_defensible_reading`): Bases have 0 AP and never deal battle damage; an attack on a player with a Base present deals the attacker's AP to the Base only (excess is lost, 5-5-6); <First Strike> changes nothing against a Base.
  - Rationale: Every printed Base lacks AP and 8-5-2-4 describes one-way damage; 13-1-5-2's mention of a Base dealing damage has no card that could use it.

### `rules-internal:remove-exile-terminology` — 'Remove', 'removed from the game' and 'exile' are used inconsistently

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: EXR-001, GD05-018
- 5-12-1 defines 'remove' as placing a card into the removal area, 5-17-4 says tokens 'removed from the game' go outside the game, 5-17-3-2-3 says a used EX Resource is 'removed from the game', 13-1-8-1 and many cards say 'exile … from the game' (never defined), and EX Resource reminder text says it is 'exiled from the game'.
- Evidence:
  - rules 5-12-1: “This describes a card being placed from any location into the removal area.”
  - rules 5-17-4: “Tokens removed from the game are placed outside the game, and not in the removal area.”
  - rules 5-17-3-2-3: “When an EX Resource is used to pay a cost, that EX Resource is removed from the game.”
  - rules 13-1-8-1: “you may exile (number) of (G Generation) cards in your trash from the game”
  - card EXR-001: “(Rest an EX Resource then exile it from the game when paying a cost.)”
  - card GD05-018: “When one of your EX Resources is exiled from the game, you may choose 1 of your Units. During this turn, when it receives enemy damage, reduce it by 3.”
- Sources: Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §5-12-1; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §5-17-4; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §5-17-3-2-3; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §13-1-8-1; gcg-api card EXR-001_p5 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=EXR-001_p5 (effect); gcg-api card GD05-018 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD05-018 (effect)
- **Resolution** (`most_defensible_reading`): 'Exile (from the game)', 'remove' and 'removed from the game' all mean: a card goes to its owner's removal area; a token (including EX Resources/EX Bases) instead leaves the game. An EX Resource used to pay a cost is 'exiled' for GD05-018's trigger.
  - Rationale: Card rulings Q194/Q211/Q213/Q220/Q226/Q229/Q232 place exiled cards in the removal area 'not including tokens', and 5-17-4 keeps tokens out of it.

### `rules-internal:when-linked-set` — 【When Linked】 triggers when a linking Pilot is 'set'

- severity **info**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: —
- 13-2-11-1 defines 【When Linked】 as activating 'when a pilot that meets the link condition is set'; 'set' is not a defined term (pairing is 5-9).
- Evidence:
  - rules 13-2-11-1: “is a keyword that indicates an effect activates when a pilot that meets the link condition is set.”
  - rules 5-9-1: “This describes placing a Pilot card, or a Command card with a 【Pilot】 effect, beneath a Unit.”
- Sources: Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §13-2-11-1; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §5-9-1
- **Resolution** (`rules_obvious_reading`): 【When Linked】 triggers when a Pilot that satisfies the Unit's link condition is paired with it (by playing or by an effect, ruling Q184); it does not trigger when an existing pairing later starts to satisfy the condition.
  - Rationale: 'Set' is the only placement verb used for Pilots here and pairing is the only way a Pilot is placed beneath a Unit.

### `rules-internal:zero-ap-player-damage` — A 0-AP attack on a player with no shield-area cards

- severity **info**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: —
- 8-5-2-2 says the attacker deals battle damage equal to its AP to the player and 'a player receiving damage is immediately defeated', but 5-5-5 says no damage is dealt when the amount is zero.
- Evidence:
  - rules 8-5-2-2: “A player receiving damage is immediately defeated”
  - rules 5-5-5: “Damage is not dealt when the amount of damage dealt would be zero.”
  - faq Q35: “Can an attack from a Unit with 0 AP destroy an opponent's Shield?”
  - faq Q35: “No, it cannot. Each Shield is treated as having 1 HP.”
- Sources: Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §8-5-2-2; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §5-5-5; Rules FAQ Q35 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/rules/faqs/list.php?sub_category=Main%20Phase%3A%20Unit%20Attacks
- **Resolution** (`rules_obvious_reading`): A Unit with 0 AP that attacks a player with no Base and no Shields deals no damage, so the player is not defeated.
  - Rationale: 5-5-5 is general and FAQ Q35 applies the same logic to Shields (0 AP cannot destroy a Shield).


## Card rulings vs card text (192)

### `ruling:EB01-010:Q315` — EB01-010 Gundam Barbatos 6th Form: <Development> may be paid with no target for ■

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: EB01-010
- Card text: “【Deploy・Development 3】You may exile the specified number of (G Generation) cards in your trash from the game. If you do, activate the following effect:” Ruling Q315 (June 12, 2026): “Can I exile the appropriate cards and activate this【Deploy・Development 3】 effect even if there are no rested enemy Units in play?” — “Yes, you can.” Unlike 10-2-2, the exile may be paid even when the ■ effect has no target.
- Evidence:
  - card EB01-010: “【Deploy・Development 3】You may exile the specified number of (G Generation) cards in your trash from the game. If you do, activate the following effect:”
  - ruling EB01-010:Q315: “Can I exile the appropriate cards and activate this【Deploy・Development 3】 effect even if there are no rested enemy Units in play?”
  - ruling EB01-010:Q315: “Yes, you can.”
  - rules 13-1-8-1: “you may exile (number) of (G Generation) cards in your trash from the game. If you do, activate the following effect.”
- Sources: gcg-api card EB01-010 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=EB01-010 (effect); Card ruling EB01-010:Q315 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=EB01-010; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §13-1-8-1
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): The <Development> exile may be performed even if the following ■ effect has no legal target; then the ■ effect does nothing.
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q315 (June 12, 2026) is authoritative for EB01-010; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

### `ruling:EB01-059:Q322` — EB01-059 Psycho Zaku: target may be chosen even if the action does nothing

- severity **info**; origin curated
- Cards: EB01-059
- Card text: “【During Link】【Attack】【Once per Turn】All players each choose 1 of their Resources. Set them as active.” Ruling Q322 (June 12, 2026): “Can this effect choose an active Resource?” — “Yes, it can. However, nothing occurs since it is already active.” Choosing a target does not require the action to change anything.
- Evidence:
  - card EB01-059: “【During Link】【Attack】【Once per Turn】All players each choose 1 of their Resources. Set them as active.”
  - ruling EB01-059:Q322: “Can this effect choose an active Resource?”
  - ruling EB01-059:Q322: “Yes, it can. However, nothing occurs since it is already active.”
  - rules 1-3-2-1: “If you are required to put an entity into some state that entity is already in, the entity remains in the state it was in, and the action putting it into that state is not performed.”
- Sources: gcg-api card EB01-059 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=EB01-059 (effect); Card ruling EB01-059:Q322 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=EB01-059; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §1-3-2-1
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): Targets may be chosen even when the action on them would be a no-op (already active, undamaged, already has the keyword); the no-op part is simply not performed.
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q322 (June 12, 2026) is authoritative for EB01-059; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

### `ruling:EB01-059:Q323` — EB01-059 Psycho Zaku: target may be chosen even if the action does nothing

- severity **info**; origin curated
- Cards: EB01-059
- Card text: “【During Link】【Attack】【Once per Turn】All players each choose 1 of their Resources. Set them as active.” Ruling Q323 (June 12, 2026): “Can this effect choose an active EX Resource?” — “Yes. It can choose an EX Resource as a Resource. However, nothing occurs since it is already active.” Choosing a target does not require the action to change anything.
- Evidence:
  - card EB01-059: “【During Link】【Attack】【Once per Turn】All players each choose 1 of their Resources. Set them as active.”
  - ruling EB01-059:Q323: “Can this effect choose an active EX Resource?”
  - ruling EB01-059:Q323: “Yes. It can choose an EX Resource as a Resource. However, nothing occurs since it is already active.”
  - rules 1-3-2-1: “If you are required to put an entity into some state that entity is already in, the entity remains in the state it was in, and the action putting it into that state is not performed.”
- Sources: gcg-api card EB01-059 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=EB01-059 (effect); Card ruling EB01-059:Q323 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=EB01-059; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §1-3-2-1
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): EX Resources are Resources for 'your Resources' choices; choosing an active one does nothing.
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q323 (June 12, 2026) is authoritative for EB01-059; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

### `ruling:EB01-060:Q324` — EB01-060 Gundam Aquarius: <Development> may be paid with no target for ■

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: EB01-060
- Card text: “【When Paired・Development 3】You may exile the specified number of (G Generation) cards in your trash from the game. If you do, activate the following effect:” Ruling Q324 (June 12, 2026): “Can I exile the appropriate cards and activate this 【When Paired・Development 3】 effect even if there are no enemy Units that are Lv.4 or lower in play?” — “Yes, you can.” Unlike 10-2-2, the exile may be paid even when the ■ effect has no target.
- Evidence:
  - card EB01-060: “【When Paired・Development 3】You may exile the specified number of (G Generation) cards in your trash from the game. If you do, activate the following effect:”
  - ruling EB01-060:Q324: “Can I exile the appropriate cards and activate this 【When Paired・Development 3】 effect even if there are no enemy Units that are Lv.4 or lower in play?”
  - ruling EB01-060:Q324: “Yes, you can.”
  - rules 13-1-8-1: “you may exile (number) of (G Generation) cards in your trash from the game. If you do, activate the following effect.”
- Sources: gcg-api card EB01-060 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=EB01-060 (effect); Card ruling EB01-060:Q324 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=EB01-060; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §13-1-8-1
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): The <Development> exile may be performed even if the following ■ effect has no legal target; then the ■ effect does nothing.
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q324 (June 12, 2026) is authoritative for EB01-060; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

### `ruling:EB01-072:Q327` — EB01-072 Yuu Kajima: every target of a multi-target choice is required

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: EB01-072
- Card text: “【When Paired】Choose 1 active friendly Unit with <Blocker> and 1 enemy Unit that is Lv.4 or lower. Rest them.” Ruling Q327 (June 12, 2026): “Can I activate this effect and only choose an enemy Unit if I don't have a Unit in play?” — “No. It will only activate if you can choose both Units.” Rule 1-3-2 suggests resolving as much as possible; the ruling applies 10-2-2 to the whole sentence instead.
- Evidence:
  - card EB01-072: “【When Paired】Choose 1 active friendly Unit with <Blocker> and 1 enemy Unit that is Lv.4 or lower. Rest them.”
  - ruling EB01-072:Q327: “Can I activate this effect and only choose an enemy Unit if I don't have a Unit in play?”
  - ruling EB01-072:Q327: “No. It will only activate if you can choose both Units.”
  - rules 1-3-2: “If a player is required to perform an action and only part of that action is not possible, the player must perform as much of the action as possible.”
  - rules 10-2-2: “If an effect requires choosing a target, that effect will not activate if the target cannot be chosen.”
- Sources: gcg-api card EB01-072 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=EB01-072 (effect); Card ruling EB01-072:Q327 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=EB01-072; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §1-3-2; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §10-2-2
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): 'Choose 1 X and 1 Y' activates (or the Command can be played) only if both targets can be chosen; it never resolves with just one.
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q327 (June 12, 2026) is authoritative for EB01-072; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

### `ruling:GD01-001:Q119` — GD01-001 Gundam: the card itself satisfies 'your Units' references

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: GD01-001
- Card text: “All your (White Base Team) Units gain <Repair 1>.” Ruling Q119 (July 11, 2025): “Does this Unit also gain <Repair: 1> from the first effect?” — “Yes, it does.” The rulings confirm the source card counts for its own 'all your Units' / 'one of your Units is deployed' wording.
- Evidence:
  - card GD01-001: “All your (White Base Team) Units gain <Repair 1>.”
  - ruling GD01-001:Q119: “Does this Unit also gain <Repair: 1> from the first effect?”
  - ruling GD01-001:Q119: “Yes, it does.”
  - rules 10-1-5-4: “Constant effects do not wait to be triggered or activated. They are active from the moment they enter the location where they activate.”
- Sources: gcg-api card GD01-001 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-001 (effect); Card ruling GD01-001:Q119 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-001; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §10-1-5-4
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): 'All your (X) Units' and 'when one of your (X) Units is deployed' include the source card itself; constant effects (including trait grants) apply before trigger conditions of the entering card are checked.
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q119 (July 11, 2025) is authoritative for GD01-001; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

### `ruling:GD01-003:Q121` — GD01-003 Unicorn Gundam 02 Banshee (Destroy Mode): 'choose N cards' needs exactly N

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: GD01-003
- Card text: “【During Link】【Attack】Choose 12 cards from your trash. Return them to their owner's deck and shuffle it. If you do, set this Unit as active. It gains <First Strike> during this turn.” Ruling Q121 (July 10, 2026): “If I only have 10 cards in my trash, can I still choose those 10 cards and activate this 【During Link】【Attack】 effect?” — “No. You cannot activate it if you cannot choose 12 cards.” Choosing cards from the trash is choosing targets (10-2-2-1); fewer than N valid cards means the effect cannot be activated at all.
- Evidence:
  - card GD01-003: “【During Link】【Attack】Choose 12 cards from your trash. Return them to their owner's deck and shuffle it. If you do, set this Unit as active. It gains <First Strike> during this turn.”
  - ruling GD01-003:Q121: “If I only have 10 cards in my trash, can I still choose those 10 cards and activate this 【During Link】【Attack】 effect?”
  - ruling GD01-003:Q121: “No. You cannot activate it if you cannot choose 12 cards.”
  - rules 1-3-2: “If a player is required to perform an action and only part of that action is not possible, the player must perform as much of the action as possible.”
- Sources: gcg-api card GD01-003 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-003 (effect); Card ruling GD01-003:Q121 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-003; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §1-3-2
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): An effect that chooses N cards from a public location activates only if N valid cards can be chosen; no partial resolution.
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q121 (July 10, 2026) is authoritative for GD01-003; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

### `ruling:GD01-019:Q124` — GD01-019 Byarlant Custom: block stands after <Blocker> is lost

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: GD01-019
- Card text: “While 4 or more enemy Units are in play, this Unit gains <Blocker>.” Ruling Q124 (July 11, 2025): “This Unit gains <Blocker> while this effect's conditions are satisfied and blocks an enemy Unit. Then, the effect's conditions become unsatisfied, and this Unit loses <Blocker>. At this time, what happens to this Unit's block?” — “Since the block is already established, the two Units continue their battle.” The rules do not say what happens to an established block when the blocker later loses <Blocker>.
- Evidence:
  - card GD01-019: “While 4 or more enemy Units are in play, this Unit gains <Blocker>.”
  - ruling GD01-019:Q124: “This Unit gains <Blocker> while this effect's conditions are satisfied and blocks an enemy Unit. Then, the effect's conditions become unsatisfied, and this Unit loses <Blocker>. At this time, what happens to this Unit's block?”
  - ruling GD01-019:Q124: “Since the block is already established, the two Units continue their battle.”
  - rules 8-3-1: “When activated, this effect changes the attack target of the attacking Unit to the Unit with <Blocker>.”
- Sources: gcg-api card GD01-019 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-019 (effect); Card ruling GD01-019:Q124 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-019; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §8-3-1
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): Once <Blocker> has changed the attack target, the new target stays the attack target for the rest of the battle even if the blocker loses <Blocker>.
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q124 (July 11, 2025) is authoritative for GD01-019; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

### `ruling:GD01-023:Q125` — GD01-023 Char's Gelgoog: card put into play by this effect is not paid for

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: GD01-023
- Card text: “【Activate･Main】Discard 1 (Zeon)/(Neo Zeon) Unit card：If a Pilot is not paired with this Unit, choose 1 (Newtype) Pilot card that is Lv.3 or lower from your trash. Pair it with this Unit.” Ruling Q125 (July 11, 2025): “If I pair a Pilot with this Unit using its 【Activate･Main】 effect, do I have to pay that Pilot's cost?” — “No, you do not.” The rules only describe paying a cost (and checking Lv.) when a card is played from the hand; the ruling fixes that a card deployed or paired by an effect is not paid for.
- Evidence:
  - card GD01-023: “【Activate･Main】Discard 1 (Zeon)/(Neo Zeon) Unit card：If a Pilot is not paired with this Unit, choose 1 (Newtype) Pilot card that is Lv.3 or lower from your trash. Pair it with this Unit.”
  - ruling GD01-023:Q125: “If I pair a Pilot with this Unit using its 【Activate･Main】 effect, do I have to pay that Pilot's cost?”
  - ruling GD01-023:Q125: “No, you do not.”
  - rules 5-7-1: “This typically describes revealing a card in your hand and paying its cost to use it.”
  - rules 7-5-2-2-3: “Choose the number of Resources necessary to pay its cost and rest them.”
- Sources: gcg-api card GD01-023 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-023 (effect); Card ruling GD01-023:Q125 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-023; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §5-7-1; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §7-5-2-2-3
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): A card deployed, paired or activated by an effect pays no cost and needs no Lv. check unless the text says 'Pay its cost'; its own 【Deploy】/【When Paired】/【When Linked】 still trigger.
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q125 (July 11, 2025) is authoritative for GD01-023; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

### `ruling:GD01-023:Q162` — GD01-023 Char's Gelgoog: Command cards with 【Pilot】 are not 'Pilot cards'

- severity **info**; origin curated
- Cards: GD01-023
- Card text: “【Activate･Main】Discard 1 (Zeon)/(Neo Zeon) Unit card：If a Pilot is not paired with this Unit, choose 1 (Newtype) Pilot card that is Lv.3 or lower from your trash. Pair it with this Unit.” Ruling Q162 (July 25, 2025): “Can this effect choose a Command card with a (Newtype) 【Pilot】 effect that is Lv.3 or lower?” — “No, it cannot.” Consistent with 3-4-6-3, but easy to get wrong when filtering by trait.
- Evidence:
  - card GD01-023: “【Activate･Main】Discard 1 (Zeon)/(Neo Zeon) Unit card：If a Pilot is not paired with this Unit, choose 1 (Newtype) Pilot card that is Lv.3 or lower from your trash. Pair it with this Unit.”
  - ruling GD01-023:Q162: “Can this effect choose a Command card with a (Newtype) 【Pilot】 effect that is Lv.3 or lower?”
  - ruling GD01-023:Q162: “No, it cannot.”
  - rules 3-4-6-3: “It is a “Pilot” while paired and a “Command card” if it is in any other location.”
- Sources: gcg-api card GD01-023 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-023 (effect); Card ruling GD01-023:Q162 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-023; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §3-4-6-3
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): Outside the battle area a Command card with a 【Pilot】 effect is only a Command card: it never matches 'Pilot card' or 'Unit card' filters.
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q162 (July 25, 2025) is authoritative for GD01-023; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

### `ruling:GD01-025:Q127` — GD01-025 Gundam Deathscythe: 'Then' part resolves although the first part cannot

- severity **info**; origin curated
- Cards: GD01-025
- Card text: “【When Paired･(Operation Meteor) Pilot】Place 1 rested Resource. Then, this Unit gains <First Strike> during this turn.” Ruling Q127 (July 11, 2025): “Does this 【When Paired】 effect activate even if there are no Resources in my resource deck?” — “Yes, it does.” Read literally, 5-20-2 lets the 'Then' part resolve on its own; the ruling puts it inside the preceding condition.
- Evidence:
  - card GD01-025: “【When Paired･(Operation Meteor) Pilot】Place 1 rested Resource. Then, this Unit gains <First Strike> during this turn.”
  - ruling GD01-025:Q127: “Does this 【When Paired】 effect activate even if there are no Resources in my resource deck?”
  - ruling GD01-025:Q127: “Yes, it does.”
  - rules 5-20-2: “Even if the text portion preceding the word “Then” in card text text cannot be resolved, resolving the succeeding portion is possible.”
- Sources: gcg-api card GD01-025 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-025 (effect); Card ruling GD01-025:Q127 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-025; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §5-20-2
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): With no Resource left in the resource deck the 【When Paired】 effect still activates and 'Then, this Unit gains <First Strike>' still happens (5-20-2); this is the contrast case for Q175/Q233, where a leading condition or 'If you do' governs the 'Then' part.
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q127 (July 11, 2025) is authoritative for GD01-025; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

### `ruling:GD01-059:Q135` — GD01-059 Zee Zulu: condition/target restriction checked only when the effect resolves

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: GD01-059
- Card text: “【Attack】If you are attacking the enemy player, this Unit gets AP+2 during this battle.” Ruling Q135 (July 11, 2025): “If this Unit is blocked by an enemy Unit after it attacks the enemy player, does its 【Attack】 effect remain in effect?” — “Yes, it does.” Rules do not say whether a resolved effect is undone when its condition or target restriction stops being true.
- Evidence:
  - card GD01-059: “【Attack】If you are attacking the enemy player, this Unit gets AP+2 during this battle.”
  - ruling GD01-059:Q135: “If this Unit is blocked by an enemy Unit after it attacks the enemy player, does its 【Attack】 effect remain in effect?”
  - ruling GD01-059:Q135: “Yes, it does.”
  - rules 10-3-3: “those targets are chosen at the point in time when the instructions appear in the effect.”
- Sources: gcg-api card GD01-059 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-059 (effect); Card ruling GD01-059:Q135 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-059; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §10-3-3
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): Conditions and target restrictions of triggered/activated/command effects are evaluated once, when resolved; later changes neither undo the result nor retrigger it.
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q135 (July 11, 2025) is authoritative for GD01-059; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

### `ruling:GD01-094:Q149` — GD01-094 Yzak Jule: destruction by damage vs by a 'destroy' effect

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: GD01-094
- Card text: “【Once per Turn】 When an enemy Link Unit is destroyed with damage while this Unit is attacking, draw 1.” Ruling Q149 (July 11, 2025): “If I destroy a damaged Link Unit with an effect that says to "destroy" a Unit, does this effect activate?” — “No, it does not.” The rules use 'destroy' for both damage and effect destruction; rulings separate them.
- Evidence:
  - card GD01-094: “【Once per Turn】 When an enemy Link Unit is destroyed with damage while this Unit is attacking, draw 1.”
  - ruling GD01-094:Q149: “If I destroy a damaged Link Unit with an effect that says to "destroy" a Unit, does this effect activate?”
  - ruling GD01-094:Q149: “No, it does not.”
  - rules 5-10-1: “This describes cards receiving damage equal to or greater than their HP and cards being placed from the field into the trash by effects.”
- Sources: gcg-api card GD01-094 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-094 (effect); Card ruling GD01-094:Q149 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-094; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §5-10-1
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): Record the cause of every destruction (battle damage, effect damage, 'destroy' effect, rules). 'Destroyed by an effect' / 'can't be destroyed by effects' mean 'destroy' effects only; 'destroyed with damage' means battle or effect damage; 'with battle damage' means battle damage only.
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q149 (July 11, 2025) is authoritative for GD01-094; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

### `ruling:GD01-103:Q153` — GD01-103 The Stubborn Cog: every target of a multi-target choice is required

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: GD01-103
- Card text: “【Main】Choose 1 active friendly (Earth Federation) Unit and 1 active enemy Unit. Rest them.” Ruling Q153 (July 11, 2025): “Can I play this 【Main】 effect and only choose an enemy Unit if I don't have an active Unit in play?” — “No, you cannot.” Rule 1-3-2 suggests resolving as much as possible; the ruling applies 10-2-2 to the whole sentence instead.
- Evidence:
  - card GD01-103: “【Main】Choose 1 active friendly (Earth Federation) Unit and 1 active enemy Unit. Rest them.”
  - ruling GD01-103:Q153: “Can I play this 【Main】 effect and only choose an enemy Unit if I don't have an active Unit in play?”
  - ruling GD01-103:Q153: “No, you cannot.”
  - rules 1-3-2: “If a player is required to perform an action and only part of that action is not possible, the player must perform as much of the action as possible.”
  - rules 10-2-2: “If an effect requires choosing a target, that effect will not activate if the target cannot be chosen.”
- Sources: gcg-api card GD01-103 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-103 (effect); Card ruling GD01-103:Q153 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-103; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §1-3-2; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §10-2-2
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): 'Choose 1 X and 1 Y' activates (or the Command can be played) only if both targets can be chosen; it never resolves with just one.
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q153 (July 11, 2025) is authoritative for GD01-103; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

- Evidence:
  - card GD01-125: “【Deploy】Add 1 of your Shields to your hand. Then, if it is your turn, you may deploy 1 (Zeon) Unit card that is Lv.4 or lower from your hand.”
  - ruling GD01-125:Q157: “When deploying a Unit with this 【Deploy】 effect, does that Unit's cost need to be paid?”
  - ruling GD01-125:Q157: “No, it does not.”

### `ruling:GD02-002:Q197` — GD02-002 Gundam Epyon: a 【Once per Turn】 effect that does nothing is not used up

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: GD02-002
- Card text: “【During Link】【Once per Turn】During your turn, when one of your Units destroys an enemy Unit with battle damage, set this Unit as active.” Ruling Q197 (January 09, 2026): “While this Unit is active, another one of my Units destroys an enemy Unit with battle damage. At this time, does this Unit get set as active and the effect end up activated?” — “No. The Unit is already active, so setting it as active is not performed and the effect does not end up activated. This means its once per turn activation has not occurred at this time.” Choosing a target does not require the action to change anything.
- Evidence:
  - card GD02-002: “【During Link】【Once per Turn】During your turn, when one of your Units destroys an enemy Unit with battle damage, set this Unit as active.”
  - ruling GD02-002:Q197: “While this Unit is active, another one of my Units destroys an enemy Unit with battle damage. At this time, does this Unit get set as active and the effect end up activated?”
  - ruling GD02-002:Q197: “No. The Unit is already active, so setting it as active is not performed and the effect does not end up activated. This means its once per turn activation has not occurred at this time.”
  - rules 1-3-2-1: “If you are required to put an entity into some state that entity is already in, the entity remains in the state it was in, and the action putting it into that state is not performed.”
- Sources: gcg-api card GD02-002 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD02-002 (effect); Card ruling GD02-002:Q197 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD02-002; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §1-3-2-1
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): If a 【Once per Turn】 effect resolves without performing its action (e.g. setting an already-active Unit active), it does not count as activated for 【Once per Turn】 and may trigger again this turn.
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q197 (January 09, 2026) is authoritative for GD02-002; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

### `ruling:GD02-021:Q175` — GD02-021 Gundam AGE-1 Normal: scope of 'If …' / 'If you do' over a following 'Then'

- severity **major**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: GD02-021
- Card text: “【Deploy】You may discard 1 green (Earth Federation) Unit card. If you do, place 1 EX Resource. Then, if you are Lv.7 or higher, draw 1.” Ruling Q175 (October 24, 2025): “If I am Lv.7 or higher, can I draw a card with this effect without discarding one green (Earth Federation) Unit card?” — “No, you cannot.” Read literally, 5-20-2 lets the 'Then' part resolve on its own; the ruling puts it inside the preceding condition.
- Evidence:
  - card GD02-021: “【Deploy】You may discard 1 green (Earth Federation) Unit card. If you do, place 1 EX Resource. Then, if you are Lv.7 or higher, draw 1.”
  - ruling GD02-021:Q175: “If I am Lv.7 or higher, can I draw a card with this effect without discarding one green (Earth Federation) Unit card?”
  - ruling GD02-021:Q175: “No, you cannot.”
  - rules 5-20-2: “Even if the text portion preceding the word “Then” in card text text cannot be resolved, resolving the succeeding portion is possible.”
- Sources: gcg-api card GD02-021 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD02-021 (effect); Card ruling GD02-021:Q175 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD02-021; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §5-20-2
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): A leading 'If <condition>,' or 'If you do,' governs every following sentence joined by 'Then' in the same effect; 'Then' only makes the next part independent of whether the immediately preceding action succeeded.
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q175 (October 24, 2025) is authoritative for GD02-021; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

### `ruling:GD02-038:Q178` — GD02-038 GQuuuuuuX (Omega Psycommu): card put into play by this effect is not paid for

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: GD02-038
- Card text: “【Deploy】Look at the top 3 cards of your deck. You may deploy 1 (Clan) Unit card that is Lv.4 or lower among them. Return the remaining cards randomly to the bottom of your deck.” Ruling Q178 (October 24, 2025): “When deploying a Unit with this 【Deploy】 effect, does that Unit's cost need to be paid?” — “No, it does not.” The rules only describe paying a cost (and checking Lv.) when a card is played from the hand; the ruling fixes that a card deployed or paired by an effect is not paid for.
- Evidence:
  - card GD02-038: “【Deploy】Look at the top 3 cards of your deck. You may deploy 1 (Clan) Unit card that is Lv.4 or lower among them. Return the remaining cards randomly to the bottom of your deck.”
  - ruling GD02-038:Q178: “When deploying a Unit with this 【Deploy】 effect, does that Unit's cost need to be paid?”
  - ruling GD02-038:Q178: “No, it does not.”
  - rules 5-7-1: “This typically describes revealing a card in your hand and paying its cost to use it.”
  - rules 7-5-2-2-3: “Choose the number of Resources necessary to pay its cost and rest them.”
- Sources: gcg-api card GD02-038 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD02-038 (effect); Card ruling GD02-038:Q178 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD02-038; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §5-7-1; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §7-5-2-2-3
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): A card deployed, paired or activated by an effect pays no cost and needs no Lv. check unless the text says 'Pay its cost'; its own 【Deploy】/【When Paired】/【When Linked】 still trigger.
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q178 (October 24, 2025) is authoritative for GD02-038; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

### `ruling:GD02-071:Q183` — GD02-071 Gundam Mk-II (AEUG): card put into play by this effect is not paid for

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: GD02-071
- Card text: “【Deploy】If a friendly white Base is in play, you may pair 1 (AEUG) Pilot card from your hand with this Unit.” Ruling Q183 (October 24, 2025): “When I pair a Pilot with this 【Deploy】 effect, do I have to pay that Pilot's cost?” — “No, you do not.” The rules only describe paying a cost (and checking Lv.) when a card is played from the hand; the ruling fixes that a card deployed or paired by an effect is not paid for.
- Evidence:
  - card GD02-071: “【Deploy】If a friendly white Base is in play, you may pair 1 (AEUG) Pilot card from your hand with this Unit.”
  - ruling GD02-071:Q183: “When I pair a Pilot with this 【Deploy】 effect, do I have to pay that Pilot's cost?”
  - ruling GD02-071:Q183: “No, you do not.”
  - rules 5-7-1: “This typically describes revealing a card in your hand and paying its cost to use it.”
  - rules 7-5-2-2-3: “Choose the number of Resources necessary to pay its cost and rest them.”
- Sources: gcg-api card GD02-071 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD02-071 (effect); Card ruling GD02-071:Q183 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD02-071; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §5-7-1; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §7-5-2-2-3
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): A card deployed, paired or activated by an effect pays no cost and needs no Lv. check unless the text says 'Pay its cost'; its own 【Deploy】/【When Paired】/【When Linked】 still trigger.
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q183 (October 24, 2025) is authoritative for GD02-071; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

### `ruling:GD02-076:Q186` — GD02-076 Buster Gundam: block stands after <Blocker> is lost

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: GD02-076
- Card text: “While this Unit has 5 or more AP, it gains <Blocker>.” Ruling Q186 (October 24, 2025): “With the conditions for its effect fulfilled, this Unit gains <Blocker> and blocks an enemy Unit. After that, the conditions become unfulfilled and it loses <Blocker>. At this time, what happens to this Unit's block?” — “Since the block is already established, the two Units continue their battle.” The rules do not say what happens to an established block when the blocker later loses <Blocker>.
- Evidence:
  - card GD02-076: “While this Unit has 5 or more AP, it gains <Blocker>.”
  - ruling GD02-076:Q186: “With the conditions for its effect fulfilled, this Unit gains <Blocker> and blocks an enemy Unit. After that, the conditions become unfulfilled and it loses <Blocker>. At this time, what happens to this Unit's block?”
  - ruling GD02-076:Q186: “Since the block is already established, the two Units continue their battle.”
  - rules 8-3-1: “When activated, this effect changes the attack target of the attacking Unit to the Unit with <Blocker>.”
- Sources: gcg-api card GD02-076 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD02-076 (effect); Card ruling GD02-076:Q186 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD02-076; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §8-3-1
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): Once <Blocker> has changed the attack target, the new target stays the attack target for the rest of the battle even if the blocker loses <Blocker>.
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q186 (October 24, 2025) is authoritative for GD02-076; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

### `ruling:GD02-082:Q187` — GD02-082 Gaelio's Schwalbe Graze: block stands after <Blocker> is lost

After that, the conditions become unfulfilled and it loses <Blocker>. At this time, what happens to this Unit's block?” — “Since the block is already established, the two Units continue their battle.” The rules do not say what happens to an established block when the blocker later loses <Blocker>.

  - ruling GD02-082:Q187: “With the conditions for its effect fulfilled, this Unit gains <Blocker> and blocks an enemy Unit. After that, the conditions become unfulfilled and it loses <Blocker>. At this time, what happens to this Unit's block?”

- Sources: gcg-api card GD02-082 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD02-082 (effect); Card ruling GD02-082:Q187 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD02-082; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §8-3-1

### `ruling:GD02-093:Q190` — GD02-093 Olba Frost: 'destroys an enemy … with battle damage' fires when both Units are destroyed

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: GD02-093
- Card text: “During your turn, when this Unit destroys an enemy Unit paired with a (Newtype) Pilot with battle damage, draw 1.” Ruling Q190 (October 24, 2025): “Does this effect activate even when both Units are destroyed in battle?” — “Yes, it does.” The trigger source is itself destroyed in the same damage step; the ruling confirms the trigger still happens and resolves.
- Evidence:
  - card GD02-093: “During your turn, when this Unit destroys an enemy Unit paired with a (Newtype) Pilot with battle damage, draw 1.”
  - ruling GD02-093:Q190: “Does this effect activate even when both Units are destroyed in battle?”
  - ruling GD02-093:Q190: “Yes, it does.”
  - rules 8-5-3-2-3: “If both battling Units are destroyed, their destruction is treated as happening simultaneously.”
  - rules 10-1-6-4: “If a card with a triggered effect leaves the location where it is active while that effect is waiting to be activated after it was triggered, the effect still activates.”
- Sources: gcg-api card GD02-093 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD02-093 (effect); Card ruling GD02-093:Q190 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD02-093; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §8-5-3-2-3; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §10-1-6-4
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): 'When this Unit destroys an enemy … with (battle) damage' triggers even if this Unit is destroyed simultaneously; the pending trigger resolves after both Units are in the trash (parts that need this Unit on the field do nothing).
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q190 (October 24, 2025) is authoritative for GD02-093; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

### `ruling:GD02-106:Q192` — GD02-106 White Wolf: <Breach> damage is effect damage from the Unit

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: GD02-106
- Card text: “【Action】During this battle, your shield area cards can't receive damage from enemy Units that are Lv.3 or lower.” Ruling Q192 (October 24, 2025): “Does "your shield area cards can't receive damage from enemy Units that are Lv.3 or lower" mean they can't receive battle damage or damage from effects like <Breach>?” — “Yes, that is correct.” The rules never classify <Breach> damage; rulings treat it as effect damage dealt by the Unit that has <Breach>.
- Evidence:
  - card GD02-106: “【Action】During this battle, your shield area cards can't receive damage from enemy Units that are Lv.3 or lower.”
  - ruling GD02-106:Q192: “Does "your shield area cards can't receive damage from enemy Units that are Lv.3 or lower" mean they can't receive battle damage or damage from effects like <Breach>?”
  - ruling GD02-106:Q192: “Yes, that is correct.”
  - rules 13-1-2-1: “deals (amount) damage to the first card in the shield area of the opponent who owns the destroyed Unit.”
- Sources: gcg-api card GD02-106 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD02-106 (effect); Card ruling GD02-106:Q192 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD02-106; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §13-1-2-1
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): <Breach> damage, and any damage dealt by a Unit's own effects (including effects it has from its Pilot), is effect damage attributed to that Unit: 'battle damage' prevention and triggers ignore it; 'damage from enemy Units' prevention and effect-damage reduction apply to it; 'when this Unit destroys … with damage' counts it.
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q192 (October 24, 2025) is authoritative for GD02-106; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

### `ruling:GD02-111:Q194` — GD02-111 Decisive Last Resort: 'exile' puts cards in the removal area

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: GD02-111
- Card text: “【Main】Choose 6 purple Unit cards from your trash. Exile them from the game. If you do, choose 1 enemy Unit. Destroy it.” Ruling Q194 (October 24, 2025): “Where are the cards exiled by this effect placed?” — “In the removal area. Since this is separate from the trash, set aside a location where only exiled cards are placed (not including tokens).” 'Exile (from the game)' is not defined in the rules; the ruling maps it to the removal area (tokens excluded).
- Evidence:
  - card GD02-111: “【Main】Choose 6 purple Unit cards from your trash. Exile them from the game. If you do, choose 1 enemy Unit. Destroy it.”
  - ruling GD02-111:Q194: “Where are the cards exiled by this effect placed?”
  - ruling GD02-111:Q194: “In the removal area. Since this is separate from the trash, set aside a location where only exiled cards are placed (not including tokens).”
  - rules 5-12-1: “This describes a card being placed from any location into the removal area.”
- Sources: gcg-api card GD02-111 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD02-111 (effect); Card ruling GD02-111:Q194 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD02-111; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §5-12-1
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): Exiled cards go to their owner's removal area (public, 4-7); an exiled token leaves the game instead (5-17-2-5).
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q194 (October 24, 2025) is authoritative for GD02-111; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

### `ruling:GD03-001:Q209` — GD03-001 Gundam NT-1: 'when this effect destroys' means immediately

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: GD03-001
- Card text: “【When Paired】Choose 1 rested enemy Unit. Deal 1 damage to it. When this effect destroys an enemy Unit, draw 1.” Ruling Q209 (January 16, 2026): “Does this second effect's "When this effect destroys an enemy Unit, draw 1" mean I can draw even when the Unit dealt damage by this effect is destroyed later in battle or by some other means?” — “No, you cannot. You can only draw when this effect's "Choose 1 rested enemy Unit. Deal 1 damage to it" deals damage and destroys the Unit.” The ruling limits the follow-up to destruction caused directly by this effect's damage.
- Evidence:
  - card GD03-001: “【When Paired】Choose 1 rested enemy Unit. Deal 1 damage to it. When this effect destroys an enemy Unit, draw 1.”
  - ruling GD03-001:Q209: “Does this second effect's "When this effect destroys an enemy Unit, draw 1" mean I can draw even when the Unit dealt damage by this effect is destroyed later in battle or by some other means?”
  - ruling GD03-001:Q209: “No, you cannot. You can only draw when this effect's "Choose 1 rested enemy Unit. Deal 1 damage to it" deals damage and destroys the Unit.”
  - rules 10-3-1-4: “Resolve all events which occur in response to activating the effect.”
- Sources: gcg-api card GD03-001 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD03-001 (effect); Card ruling GD03-001:Q209 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD03-001; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §10-3-1-4
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): 'When this effect destroys an enemy Unit' is checked right after this effect's damage is dealt (rules management); later destruction does not count.
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q209 (January 16, 2026) is authoritative for GD03-001; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

### `ruling:GD03-019:Q214` — GD03-019 Gundam AGE-2 Normal: forced attack-target effects

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: GD03-019
- Card text: “【During Pair】Enemy Units choose this rested Unit as their attack target if possible when attacking.” Ruling Q214 (January 16, 2026): “What happens if there are multiple Units in play that attract the attack target with effects like "Enemy Units choose this rested Unit as their attack target if possible when attacking"?” — “Choose one of the Units attracting the attack target as the attack target.” The rules have no notion of forced attack targets; rulings define how they combine.
- Evidence:
  - card GD03-019: “【During Pair】Enemy Units choose this rested Unit as their attack target if possible when attacking.”
  - ruling GD03-019:Q214: “What happens if there are multiple Units in play that attract the attack target with effects like "Enemy Units choose this rested Unit as their attack target if possible when attacking"?”
  - ruling GD03-019:Q214: “Choose one of the Units attracting the attack target as the attack target.”
  - rules 8-2-1: “The attack target can be either the opposing player or a rested enemy Unit.”
- Sources: gcg-api card GD03-019 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD03-019 (effect); Card ruling GD03-019:Q214 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD03-019; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §8-2-1
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): When declaring an attack: 'must choose that Unit' (GD04-107) outranks 'choose … if possible' attractors; with several eligible forced/attracting Units the attacker picks one; a forced Unit that is not a legal target (active without an active-target permission, or gone) imposes nothing; constraints apply at declaration only and never retarget an attack in progress.
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q214 (January 16, 2026) is authoritative for GD03-019; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.
