### `ruling:ST12-016:Q452` — ST12-016 Libra: 'has destroyed … this turn' uses the state at the time

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: ST12-016
- Card text: “【Activate･Main】Rest this Base：If a friendly Unit paired with a Pilot has destroyed an enemy Unit with battle damage this turn, choose 1 enemy Unit that is Lv.4 or lower. Deal 1 damage to it.” Ruling Q452 (September 11, 2026): “Does this 【Activate･Main】 effect activate even when both Units are destroyed in battle?” — “Yes, it does.” The condition looks back at an event, so the engine must remember it with the Unit's state then.
- Evidence:
  - card ST12-016: “【Activate･Main】Rest this Base：If a friendly Unit paired with a Pilot has destroyed an enemy Unit with battle damage this turn, choose 1 enemy Unit that is Lv.4 or lower. Deal 1 damage to it.”
  - ruling ST12-016:Q452: “Does this 【Activate･Main】 effect activate even when both Units are destroyed in battle?”
  - ruling ST12-016:Q452: “Yes, it does.”
  - rules 10-2-1: “When certain conditions are required for an effect to activate, it will not activate unless those conditions are fulfilled.”
- Sources: gcg-api card ST12-016 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=ST12-016 (effect); Card ruling ST12-016:Q452 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=ST12-016; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §10-2-1
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): Keep a per-turn event log of battle destructions with the destroying Unit's pairing state at that moment; the condition is met even if that Unit is gone, and not by pairing afterwards.
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q452 (September 11, 2026) is authoritative for ST12-016; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

### `ruling:ST12-016:Q453` — ST12-016 Libra: 'has destroyed … this turn' uses the state at the time

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: ST12-016
- Card text: “【Activate･Main】Rest this Base：If a friendly Unit paired with a Pilot has destroyed an enemy Unit with battle damage this turn, choose 1 enemy Unit that is Lv.4 or lower. Deal 1 damage to it.” Ruling Q453 (September 11, 2026): “If a Pilot is paired with a friendly Unit after that Unit has destroyed an enemy Unit with battle damage, can I activate this 【Activate･Main】 effect?” — “No, you cannot.” The condition looks back at an event, so the engine must remember it with the Unit's state then.
- Evidence:
  - card ST12-016: “【Activate･Main】Rest this Base：If a friendly Unit paired with a Pilot has destroyed an enemy Unit with battle damage this turn, choose 1 enemy Unit that is Lv.4 or lower. Deal 1 damage to it.”
  - ruling ST12-016:Q453: “If a Pilot is paired with a friendly Unit after that Unit has destroyed an enemy Unit with battle damage, can I activate this 【Activate･Main】 effect?”
  - ruling ST12-016:Q453: “No, you cannot.”
  - rules 10-2-1: “When certain conditions are required for an effect to activate, it will not activate unless those conditions are fulfilled.”
- Sources: gcg-api card ST12-016 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=ST12-016 (effect); Card ruling ST12-016:Q453 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=ST12-016; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §10-2-1
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): Keep a per-turn event log of battle destructions with the destroying Unit's pairing state at that moment; the condition is met even if that Unit is gone, and not by pairing afterwards.
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q453 (September 11, 2026) is authoritative for ST12-016; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

### `ruling:ST13-001:Q455` — ST13-001 Qubeley: battles begun by an effect

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: ST13-001
- Card text: “【Activate･Main】【Once per Turn】①：Choose 1 of your Unit tokens and 1 enemy Unit. Begin a battle between them and only perform the damage step.” Ruling Q455 (September 11, 2026): “When I use this effect to begin a battle with an enemy GD02-073 Carta's Graze Ritter (Ground Type), does its "During your opponent's turn, the enemy Unit battling this Unit gains <First Strike>" effect activate?” — “Yes, it does. Effects that activate during battle apart from effects like 【Attack】 and "When [...] attacks" activate even during battles initiated by effects.” The rules only say attack triggers are skipped; the rulings confirm everything else about a battle applies.
- Evidence:
  - card ST13-001: “【Activate･Main】【Once per Turn】①：Choose 1 of your Unit tokens and 1 enemy Unit. Begin a battle between them and only perform the damage step.”
  - ruling ST13-001:Q455: “When I use this effect to begin a battle with an enemy GD02-073 Carta's Graze Ritter (Ground Type), does its "During your opponent's turn, the enemy Unit battling this Unit gains <First Strike>" effect activate?”
  - ruling ST13-001:Q455: “Yes, it does. Effects that activate during battle apart from effects like 【Attack】 and "When [...] attacks" activate even during battles initiated by effects.”
  - rules 5-22-3-1: “Since the attack step was skipped, effects such as 【Attack】 and "when a Unit attacks" are not triggered.”
- Sources: gcg-api card ST13-001 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=ST13-001 (effect); Card ruling ST13-001:Q455 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=ST13-001; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §5-22-3-1
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): A battle begun by an effect runs only the damage step and battle end step; 'battling'-conditioned effects and <First Strike> apply, destruction counts as destruction with battle damage (<Breach> etc.), only 【Attack】/'when … attacks' are skipped.
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q455 (September 11, 2026) is authoritative for ST13-001; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

### `ruling:ST13-001:Q456` — ST13-001 Qubeley: battles begun by an effect

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: ST13-001
- Card text: “【Activate･Main】【Once per Turn】①：Choose 1 of your Unit tokens and 1 enemy Unit. Begin a battle between them and only perform the damage step.” Ruling Q456 (September 11, 2026): “When I destroy an enemy Unit in a battle initiated by this effect, does that fall within the criterion "when this Unit destroys an enemy Unit with battle damage"?” — “Yes, it does.” The rules only say attack triggers are skipped; the rulings confirm everything else about a battle applies.
- Evidence:
  - card ST13-001: “【Activate･Main】【Once per Turn】①：Choose 1 of your Unit tokens and 1 enemy Unit. Begin a battle between them and only perform the damage step.”
  - ruling ST13-001:Q456: “When I destroy an enemy Unit in a battle initiated by this effect, does that fall within the criterion "when this Unit destroys an enemy Unit with battle damage"?”
  - ruling ST13-001:Q456: “Yes, it does.”
  - rules 5-22-3-1: “Since the attack step was skipped, effects such as 【Attack】 and "when a Unit attacks" are not triggered.”
- Sources: gcg-api card ST13-001 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=ST13-001 (effect); Card ruling ST13-001:Q456 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=ST13-001; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §5-22-3-1
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): A battle begun by an effect runs only the damage step and battle end step; 'battling'-conditioned effects and <First Strike> apply, destruction counts as destruction with battle damage (<Breach> etc.), only 【Attack】/'when … attacks' are skipped.
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q456 (September 11, 2026) is authoritative for ST13-001; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

### `ruling:ST13-012:Q459` — ST13-012 Suletta Mercury: destruction by damage vs by a 'destroy' effect

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: ST13-012
- Card text: “【Once per Turn】When an enemy Unit is destroyed with effect damage while this Unit is attacking, draw 1.” Ruling Q459 (September 11, 2026): “If I destroy a damaged Link Unit with an effect that says to "destroy" a Unit, does this effect activate?” — “No, it does not.” The rules use 'destroy' for both damage and effect destruction; rulings separate them.
- Evidence:
  - card ST13-012: “【Once per Turn】When an enemy Unit is destroyed with effect damage while this Unit is attacking, draw 1.”
  - ruling ST13-012:Q459: “If I destroy a damaged Link Unit with an effect that says to "destroy" a Unit, does this effect activate?”
  - ruling ST13-012:Q459: “No, it does not.”
  - rules 5-10-1: “This describes cards receiving damage equal to or greater than their HP and cards being placed from the field into the trash by effects.”
- Sources: gcg-api card ST13-012 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=ST13-012 (effect); Card ruling ST13-012:Q459 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=ST13-012; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §5-10-1
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): Record the cause of every destruction (battle damage, effect damage, 'destroy' effect, rules). 'Destroyed by an effect' / 'can't be destroyed by effects' mean 'destroy' effects only; 'destroyed with damage' means battle or effect damage; 'with battle damage' means battle damage only.
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q459 (September 11, 2026) is authoritative for ST13-012; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

### `ruling:ST13-014:Q460` — ST13-014 Final Duty: card put into play by this effect is not paid for

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: ST13-014
- Card text: “【Main】Choose 1 of your Units. Destroy it. If you do, look at the top 4 cards of your deck. You may deploy 1 Unit card that is Lv.4 or lower among them. Return the remaining cards randomly to the bottom of your deck.” Ruling Q460 (September 11, 2026): “When deploying a Unit with this effect, do I have to pay that Unit's cost?” — “No, you do not.” The rules only describe paying a cost (and checking Lv.) when a card is played from the hand; the ruling fixes that a card deployed or paired by an effect is not paid for.
- Evidence:
  - card ST13-014: “【Main】Choose 1 of your Units. Destroy it. If you do, look at the top 4 cards of your deck. You may deploy 1 Unit card that is Lv.4 or lower among them. Return the remaining cards randomly to the bottom of your deck.”
  - ruling ST13-014:Q460: “When deploying a Unit with this effect, do I have to pay that Unit's cost?”
  - ruling ST13-014:Q460: “No, you do not.”
  - rules 5-7-1: “This typically describes revealing a card in your hand and paying its cost to use it.”
  - rules 7-5-2-2-3: “Choose the number of Resources necessary to pay its cost and rest them.”
- Sources: gcg-api card ST13-014 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=ST13-014 (effect); Card ruling ST13-014:Q460 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=ST13-014; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §5-7-1; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §7-5-2-2-3
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): A card deployed, paired or activated by an effect pays no cost and needs no Lv. check unless the text says 'Pay its cost'; its own 【Deploy】/【When Paired】/【When Linked】 still trigger.
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q460 (September 11, 2026) is authoritative for ST13-014; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

### `ruling:ST14-001:Q462` — ST14-001 The-O: ties for highest/lowest

- severity **info**; origin curated
- Cards: ST14-001
- Card text: “Each enemy player's rested Units with the lowest Lv. won't be set as active during the start phase of their turn.” Ruling Q462 (September 11, 2026): “If I use this second effect when there are two Units that are Lv.3 and one Unit that is Lv.5 on my opponent's field, the two Units that are Lv.3 won't be set as active during the start phase of their turn, right?” — “Yes. If there are multiple Units with the lowest Lv., none of them will be set as active during the start phase of the opponent's turn.” The text does not say how ties are handled.
- Evidence:
  - card ST14-001: “Each enemy player's rested Units with the lowest Lv. won't be set as active during the start phase of their turn.”
  - ruling ST14-001:Q462: “If I use this second effect when there are two Units that are Lv.3 and one Unit that is Lv.5 on my opponent's field, the two Units that are Lv.3 won't be set as active during the start phase of their turn, right?”
  - ruling ST14-001:Q462: “Yes. If there are multiple Units with the lowest Lv., none of them will be set as active during the start phase of the opponent's turn.”
  - rules 1-3-4: “When both players are required for any reason to make a selection simultaneously, the active player chooses first.”
- Sources: gcg-api card ST14-001 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=ST14-001 (effect); Card ruling ST14-001:Q462 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=ST14-001; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §1-3-4
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): 'Choose 1 … with the highest/lowest X' lets the chooser pick any tied Unit; non-choosing wording ('Units with the lowest Lv.') affects all tied Units.
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q462 (September 11, 2026) is authoritative for ST14-001; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

### `ruling:ST14-005:Q466` — ST14-005 G-Falcon DX: condition/target restriction checked only when the effect resolves

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: ST14-005
- Card text: “【Deploy】Choose 1 enemy Unit that is Lv.6 or lower. Rest it. Then, if there are 7 or more cards in your trash, it gets AP-2 during this turn.” Ruling Q466 (September 11, 2026): “If there are seven or more cards in my trash and enemy Units that are Lv.6 or less that I want to give AP-2 to that are already rested, can I choose one of those Units with this 【Deploy】 effect and give it AP-2 during this turn?” — “Yes, you can give it AP-2.” Rules do not say whether a resolved effect is undone when its condition or target restriction stops being true.
- Evidence:
  - card ST14-005: “【Deploy】Choose 1 enemy Unit that is Lv.6 or lower. Rest it. Then, if there are 7 or more cards in your trash, it gets AP-2 during this turn.”
  - ruling ST14-005:Q466: “If there are seven or more cards in my trash and enemy Units that are Lv.6 or less that I want to give AP-2 to that are already rested, can I choose one of those Units with this 【Deploy】 effect and give it AP-2 during this turn?”
  - ruling ST14-005:Q466: “Yes, you can give it AP-2.”
  - rules 10-3-3: “those targets are chosen at the point in time when the instructions appear in the effect.”
- Sources: gcg-api card ST14-005 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=ST14-005 (effect); Card ruling ST14-005:Q466 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=ST14-005; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §10-3-3
- **Resolution** (`ruling_overrides_text`): Conditions and target restrictions of triggered/activated/command effects are evaluated once, when resolved; later changes neither undo the result nor retrigger it.
  - Rationale: Official card ruling Q466 (September 11, 2026) is authoritative for ST14-005; the same reading is applied to identical wording on other cards.

## Rules FAQ vs rules (16)

### `faq:Q8` — Shield order: the top deck card becomes the bottom Shield

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: —
- Rules FAQ Q8 (2025-07-04): “Do cards placed into the shield section of the shield area have a particular order?” — “Yes. Place them so that the top card from your deck becomes the bottom Shield.” 6-2-2 places cards one at a time but never says which end of the stack is the 'top'/'first' Shield used by 4-6-4-1, 8-5-2-3 and 13-1-2-2.
- Evidence:
  - faq Q8: “Do cards placed into the shield section of the shield area have a particular order?”
  - faq Q8: “Yes. Place them so that the top card from your deck becomes the bottom Shield.”
  - rules 6-2-2: “Each player takes the top six cards of their deck, one at a time, and places them face down into their shield section without looking at them.”
- Sources: Rules FAQ Q8 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/rules/faqs/list.php?sub_category=Preparing%20to%20Play; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §6-2-2
- **Resolution** (`faq_clarifies_rules`): Shields are a stack filled from the deck top: the deck's top card is the bottom Shield; the sixth card placed is the top ('first', 'topmost') Shield, damaged and taken first.
  - Rationale: Official rules FAQ Q8 (2025-07-04) clarifies the comprehensive rules and is followed as written.

### `faq:Q15` — Player One draws on the first turn

- severity **info**; origin curated
- Cards: —
- Rules FAQ Q15 (2025-07-04): “Can Player One draw a card during the draw phase of their first turn?” — “Yes, they can.” Many card games skip the first draw; this one does not.
- Evidence:
  - faq Q15: “Can Player One draw a card during the draw phase of their first turn?”
  - faq Q15: “Yes, they can.”
  - rules 7-3-1: “The active player draws one card from their deck and adds it to their hand.”
- Sources: Rules FAQ Q15 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/rules/faqs/list.php?sub_category=Draw%20Phase; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §7-3-1
- **Resolution** (`faq_clarifies_rules`): No first-turn draw skip for Player One.
  - Rationale: Official rules FAQ Q15 (2025-07-04) clarifies the comprehensive rules and is followed as written.

### `faq:Q19` — Resource area holds 15 cards in total, at most 5 EX Resources

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: —
- Rules FAQ Q19 (2025-07-04): “Is there a maximum number of cards that can be in the resource area?” — “Yes, there is. Up to 10 Resource cards from the resource deck and 5 EX Resource cards can be placed into the resource area, for a total of 15 cards.” Read alone, 4-4-2 and 4-4-2-1 could allow 15 Resources plus 5 EX Resources.
- Evidence:
  - faq Q19: “Is there a maximum number of cards that can be in the resource area?”
  - faq Q19: “Yes, there is. Up to 10 Resource cards from the resource deck and 5 EX Resource cards can be placed into the resource area, for a total of 15 cards.”
  - rules 4-4-2: “You may have up to 15 Resources in your resource area.”
  - rules 4-4-2-1: “You may have up to five EX Resources in your resource area.”
- Sources: Rules FAQ Q19 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/rules/faqs/list.php?sub_category=Resource%20Phase; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §4-4-2; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §4-4-2-1
- **Resolution** (`faq_clarifies_rules`): At most 15 cards in the resource area including at most 5 EX Resources; a placement beyond either limit is not performed (1-3-2).
  - Rationale: Official rules FAQ Q19 (2025-07-04) clarifies the comprehensive rules and is followed as written.

### `faq:Q30` — 【Activate･Main】 on the turn a Unit is deployed

- severity **info**; origin curated
- Cards: —
- Rules FAQ Q30 (2025-07-04): “Can I activate the 【Activate･Main】 effect on a Unit during the same turn it was deployed?” — “Yes, you can.” The deploy-turn restriction covers attacking only.
- Evidence:
  - faq Q30: “Can I activate the 【Activate･Main】 effect on a Unit during the same turn it was deployed?”
  - faq Q30: “Yes, you can.”
  - rules 3-2-4: “Unless specified otherwise, a newly deployed Unit cannot attack on the turn it is deployed.”
- Sources: Rules FAQ Q30 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/rules/faqs/list.php?sub_category=Main%20Phase%3A%20Activating%20Card%20Effects; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §3-2-4
- **Resolution** (`faq_clarifies_rules`): A Unit deployed this turn may use 【Activate･Main】 effects, including rest costs such as <Support>.
  - Rationale: Official rules FAQ Q30 (2025-07-04) clarifies the comprehensive rules and is followed as written.

### `faq:Q31` — Player One may attack on the first turn

- severity **info**; origin curated
- Cards: —
- Rules FAQ Q31 (2025-07-04): “Can Player One attack during their first turn?” — “Yes, they can. However, Units cannot immediately attack during the turn in which they are deployed.” No first-turn attack restriction exists beyond the deploy-turn rule.
- Evidence:
  - faq Q31: “Can Player One attack during their first turn?”
  - faq Q31: “Yes, they can. However, Units cannot immediately attack during the turn in which they are deployed.”
  - rules 6-2-5: “The game begins with Player One’s turn.”
- Sources: Rules FAQ Q31 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/rules/faqs/list.php?sub_category=Main%20Phase%3A%20Unit%20Attacks; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §6-2-5
- **Resolution** (`faq_clarifies_rules`): No first-turn attack ban; Units deployed on an earlier turn (none on turn 1) or Link Units may attack.
  - Rationale: Official rules FAQ Q31 (2025-07-04) clarifies the comprehensive rules and is followed as written.

### `faq:Q84` — 'Friendly' equals 'your' in 1v1

- severity **info**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: —
- Rules FAQ Q84 (2025-07-04): “Is there a difference between "1 of your Units" and "1 friendly Unit"?” — “There is no difference when playing with 1v1 rules. "1 friendly Unit" means you can choose a teammate's Unit when playing with team battle rules, such as 2v2.” 'Friendly' is only defined in the team-battle rules.
- Evidence:
  - faq Q84: “Is there a difference between "1 of your Units" and "1 friendly Unit"?”
  - faq Q84: “There is no difference when playing with 1v1 rules. "1 friendly Unit" means you can choose a teammate's Unit when playing with team battle rules, such as 2v2.”
  - rules 12-3-10: “If an effect or such indicates “friendly,” it refers to both your and your teammate’s cards.”
- Sources: Rules FAQ Q84 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/rules/faqs/list.php?sub_category=Unit; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §12-3-10
- **Resolution** (`faq_clarifies_rules`): In 1v1, 'friendly' and 'your' select the same cards.
  - Rationale: Official rules FAQ Q84 (2025-07-04) clarifies the comprehensive rules and is followed as written.

### `faq:Q95` — AP below zero: clamp only the final value

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: —
- Rules FAQ Q95 (2025-07-04): “A Unit with 1 AP receives the effect "it gets AP-2" then gets AP+3 from another effect after that. At this time, what is the Unit's AP value?” — “It is the result of 1-2+3, or 2 AP.” Clamping after each modifier would give 3 AP in the FAQ's example instead of 2.
- Evidence:
  - faq Q95: “A Unit with 1 AP receives the effect "it gets AP-2" then gets AP+3 from another effect after that. At this time, what is the Unit's AP value?”
  - faq Q95: “It is the result of 1-2+3, or 2 AP.”
  - rules 1-3-6: “If the result would be negative, treat it as zero unless it will be modified by another effect.”
- Sources: Rules FAQ Q95 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/rules/faqs/list.php?sub_category=AP; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §1-3-6
- **Resolution** (`faq_clarifies_rules`): Sum all AP/HP modifiers onto the printed value and clamp the final result at 0; do not clamp intermediate results.
  - Rationale: Official rules FAQ Q95 (2025-07-04) clarifies the comprehensive rules and is followed as written.

### `faq:Q96` — HP in card text means current HP

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: —
- Rules FAQ Q96 (2025-07-04): “When an effect such as "choose 1 enemy Unit with 2 or less HP" references a card's HP, does it reference its current HP?” — “Yes, it does. If the card is damaged, reference its HP after reducing it by that damage amount.” Rules track damage as counters (5-5-1) but never say which HP a text filter reads.
- Evidence:
  - faq Q96: “When an effect such as "choose 1 enemy Unit with 2 or less HP" references a card's HP, does it reference its current HP?”
  - faq Q96: “Yes, it does. If the card is damaged, reference its HP after reducing it by that damage amount.”
  - rules 2-8-2: “A card with HP is destroyed when that HP becomes zero.”
- Sources: Rules FAQ Q96 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/rules/faqs/list.php?sub_category=HP; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §2-8-2
- **Resolution** (`faq_clarifies_rules`): HP filters and comparisons use current HP (modified HP minus damage counters).
  - Rationale: Official rules FAQ Q96 (2025-07-04) clarifies the comprehensive rules and is followed as written.

### `faq:Q97` — Battle damage is damage dealt through AP in a battle

- severity **info**; origin curated
- Cards: —
- Rules FAQ Q97 (2025-07-04): “What is "battle damage"?” — “This is damage dealt by a card's AP, typically during battle.” The FAQ's 'typically during battle' is looser than 5-5-3.
- Evidence:
  - faq Q97: “What is "battle damage"?”
  - faq Q97: “This is damage dealt by a card's AP, typically during battle.”
  - rules 5-5-3: “This damage is called battle damage.”
- Sources: Rules FAQ Q97 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/rules/faqs/list.php?sub_category=Damage; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §5-5-3
- **Resolution** (`faq_clarifies_rules`): Battle damage is only the damage the damage step deals (including battles begun by effects and battle damage redirected by 'instead' effects); effect damage never becomes battle damage even when it equals a Unit's AP.
  - Rationale: Official rules FAQ Q97 (2025-07-04) clarifies the comprehensive rules and is followed as written.

### `faq:Q98` — Unqualified 'damage' includes battle damage

- severity **info**; origin curated
- Cards: —
- Rules FAQ Q98 (2025-07-04): “Does the damage mentioned in effects like "when a Unit is destroyed with damage" include battle damage?” — “Yes, it does.” Card text that says just 'damage' covers both kinds.
- Evidence:
  - faq Q98: “Does the damage mentioned in effects like "when a Unit is destroyed with damage" include battle damage?”
  - faq Q98: “Yes, it does.”
  - rules 5-5-4: “This damage is called effect damage.”
- Sources: Rules FAQ Q98 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/rules/faqs/list.php?sub_category=Damage; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §5-5-4
- **Resolution** (`faq_clarifies_rules`): 'Damage' without a qualifier means battle or effect damage.
  - Rationale: Official rules FAQ Q98 (2025-07-04) clarifies the comprehensive rules and is followed as written.

### `faq:Q99` — Damage persists between turns

- severity **info**; origin curated
- Cards: —
- Rules FAQ Q99 (2025-07-04): “Does damage received by a Unit disappear at the end of the turn?” — “No, it does not. Damage remains on the Unit in the form of a damage counter.” No rule removes damage at end of turn.
- Evidence:
  - faq Q99: “Does damage received by a Unit disappear at the end of the turn?”
  - faq Q99: “No, it does not. Damage remains on the Unit in the form of a damage counter.”
  - rules 5-5-1-1: “Show the current amount of damage a card has received by placing a number of counters equal to that damage on top of it.”
- Sources: Rules FAQ Q99 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/rules/faqs/list.php?sub_category=Damage; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §5-5-1-1
- **Resolution** (`faq_clarifies_rules`): Damage counters stay until recovered or the card leaves the field.
  - Rationale: Official rules FAQ Q99 (2025-07-04) clarifies the comprehensive rules and is followed as written.

### `faq:Q105` — 'All your Units get AP+2 during this turn' does not reach Units deployed later

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: —
- Rules FAQ Q105 (2025-07-04): “A new Unit is deployed after the effect "all your Units get AP+2 during this turn" has activated. At this time, does the newly deployed Unit also get AP+2?” — “No, it does not. Effects that bestow some effect on all Units are only applied to Units that were in the battle area at that time.” 10-1-5-7 covers constant effects; resolved effects with a duration are not described.
- Evidence:
  - faq Q105: “A new Unit is deployed after the effect "all your Units get AP+2 during this turn" has activated. At this time, does the newly deployed Unit also get AP+2?”
  - faq Q105: “No, it does not. Effects that bestow some effect on all Units are only applied to Units that were in the battle area at that time.”
  - rules 10-1-5-7: “A constant effect with a conditional target is applied the moment a target fulfilling those conditions appears.”
- Sources: Rules FAQ Q105 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/rules/faqs/list.php?sub_category=Miscellaneous; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §10-1-5-7
- **Resolution** (`faq_clarifies_rules`): An effect that resolves once and lasts for a duration fixes its affected set at resolution; constant effects (e.g. 【During Pair】 'all your Units get AP+1') keep applying to Units that arrive later.
  - Rationale: Official rules FAQ Q105 (2025-07-04) clarifies the comprehensive rules and is followed as written.

### `faq:Q106` — Durations outlive their source

- severity **info**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: —
- Rules FAQ Q106 (2025-07-04): “After an effect which specifies "during this turn" has been activated by an effect such as 【Activate･Main】, the Unit with that 【Activate･Main】 effect is destroyed. At this time, does the effect specifying "during this turn" remain in effect?” — “Yes, it does.” Rules do not say whether a duration ends when its source leaves play.
- Evidence:
  - faq Q106: “After an effect which specifies "during this turn" has been activated by an effect such as 【Activate･Main】, the Unit with that 【Activate･Main】 effect is destroyed. At this time, does the effect specifying "during this turn" remain in effect?”
  - faq Q106: “Yes, it does.”
  - rules 7-6-6-1: “Effects with the duration limit “during this turn” lose effect.”
- Sources: Rules FAQ Q106 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/rules/faqs/list.php?sub_category=Miscellaneous; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §7-6-6-1
- **Resolution** (`faq_clarifies_rules`): 'During this turn/battle' effects last their full duration even if the card that created them leaves the field.
  - Rationale: Official rules FAQ Q106 (2025-07-04) clarifies the comprehensive rules and is followed as written.

### `faq:Q109` — Several new triggers during resolution: active player's first

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: —
- Rules FAQ Q109 (2025-07-04): “Multiple effects activate at the same time and a new effect activates while they are being resolved. At this time, how do players resolve the effects?” — “Give the new effect priority and resolve it. If multiple new effects activate simultaneously, give them priority and resolve them, starting with the active player's effects.” 10-1-6-7 covers one new trigger only.
- Evidence:
  - faq Q109: “Multiple effects activate at the same time and a new effect activates while they are being resolved. At this time, how do players resolve the effects?”
  - faq Q109: “Give the new effect priority and resolve it. If multiple new effects activate simultaneously, give them priority and resolve them, starting with the active player's effects.”
  - rules 10-1-6-7: “If a new effect triggers while multiple effects are being resolved, give that new effect priority and resolve it.”
- Sources: Rules FAQ Q109 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/rules/faqs/list.php?sub_category=Miscellaneous; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §10-1-6-7
- **Resolution** (`faq_clarifies_rules`): New triggers that occur while a batch resolves form a nested batch resolved before the rest of the outer batch: the active player's new triggers first (in their chosen order), then the standby player's; 【Burst】 still goes first (10-1-6-8).
  - Rationale: Official rules FAQ Q109 (2025-07-04) clarifies the comprehensive rules and is followed as written.

### `faq:Q195` — <Suppression>: both Shields leave together before any 【Burst】

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: —
- Rules FAQ Q195 (2026-01-09): “A Shield destroyed by <Suppression> damage is revealed to be a Base card with a 【Burst】 effect. At this time, if I add one of my Shields to my hand for the effect on the Base deployed by the 【Burst】 effect, can I add the second Shield that would be destroyed by <Suppression> to my hand?” — “No, you cannot. The Shields destroyed by <Suppression> are destroyed simultaneously. The Shield you can add to your hand for the deployed Base is the first Shield in your shield area that was not destroyed.” The rules do not say where the second destroyed Shield is while the first one's 【Burst】 resolves.
- Evidence:
  - faq Q195: “A Shield destroyed by <Suppression> damage is revealed to be a Base card with a 【Burst】 effect. At this time, if I add one of my Shields to my hand for the effect on the Base deployed by the 【Burst】 effect, can I add the second Shield that would be destroyed by <Suppression> to my hand?”
  - faq Q195: “No, you cannot. The Shields destroyed by <Suppression> are destroyed simultaneously. The Shield you can add to your hand for the deployed Base is the first Shield in your shield area that was not destroyed.”
  - rules 13-1-7-4: “When two Shields are successfully destroyed with this effect, reveal them simultaneously.”
- Sources: Rules FAQ Q195 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/rules/faqs/list.php?sub_category=Suppression; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §13-1-7-4
- **Resolution** (`faq_clarifies_rules`): Both Shields destroyed by <Suppression> leave the shield section at once (revealed, pending trash) before any 【Burst】 resolves; 'add 1 of your Shields' then takes the first Shield that was not destroyed.
  - Rationale: Official rules FAQ Q195 (2026-01-09) clarifies the comprehensive rules and is followed as written.

### `faq:Q488` — Damage reductions add up

- severity **info**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: —
- Rules FAQ Q488 (2026-09-11): “When the effects "reduce damage it receives by 2" and "reduce damage it receives by 3" both activate on the same Unit, is the damage reduced by a total of five?” — “Yes. Reduce the damage by five.” 5-21 does not cover several reductions at once.
- Evidence:
  - faq Q488: “When the effects "reduce damage it receives by 2" and "reduce damage it receives by 3" both activate on the same Unit, is the damage reduced by a total of five?”
  - faq Q488: “Yes. Reduce the damage by five.”
  - rules 5-21-1: “This refers to decreasing damage a Unit, Base, or Shield receives by the indicated amount before it is received.”
- Sources: Rules FAQ Q488 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/rules/faqs/list.php?sub_category=Fundamental%20Terminology; Comprehensive Rules Ver. 1.9.0 §5-21-1
- **Resolution** (`faq_clarifies_rules`): All applicable reductions are summed before damage is received (floor 0, 5-21-2).
  - Rationale: Official rules FAQ Q488 (2026-09-11) clarifies the comprehensive rules and is followed as written.


## Ambiguous card text (108)

### `ambiguous:EB01-001:won-t-be-set` — EB01-001 Gundam Astray Red Frame Custom (EX): reading of “It won't be set as active during the start phase of your opponent's ne…”

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: EB01-001
- Card text: “It won't be set as active during the start phase of your opponent's next turn.”. The wording admits more than one reading. The text limits the restriction to the start phase (compare rulings ST08-009:Q208 and GD04-102:Q288); a blanket 'can't be set as active' would be broader.
- Evidence:
  - card EB01-001: “It won't be set as active during the start phase of your opponent's next turn.”
- Sources: gcg-api card EB01-001 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=EB01-001 (effect)
- **Resolution** (`most_defensible_reading`): Only the untap and set-active effects during that start phase (active step and start-step triggers) are prevented. Set-active effects at any other time still work.
  - Rationale: The text limits the restriction to the start phase (compare rulings ST08-009:Q208 and GD04-102:Q288); a blanket 'can't be set as active' would be broader.

### `ambiguous:EB01-003:at-end-turn-if` — EB01-003 Narrative Gundam A-Packs (EX): reading of “At the end of your turn, if this Unit is rested, rest all Units. If th…”

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: EB01-003
- Card text: “At the end of your turn, if this Unit is rested, rest all Units. If this effect rested 3 or more Units, draw 1.”. The wording admits more than one reading. An 'If' condition is checked when the effect resolves. Resting an already-rested Unit does nothing (rule 1-3-2-1).
- Evidence:
  - card EB01-003: “At the end of your turn, if this Unit is rested, rest all Units. If this effect rested 3 or more Units, draw 1.”
- Sources: gcg-api card EB01-003 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=EB01-003 (effect)
- **Resolution** (`most_defensible_reading`): The rested check happens at resolution. 'Rested by this effect' means Units that were active just before and are rested afterwards; Units already rested do not count.
  - Rationale: An 'If' condition is checked when the effect resolves. Resting an already-rested Unit does nothing (rule 1-3-2-1).

### `ambiguous:EB01-009:all-enemy-players-each` — EB01-009 Gundam Full Armor (Thunderbolt) (EX): reading of “All enemy players each choose 1 of their active Units. Rest them.”

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: EB01-009
- Card text: “All enemy players each choose 1 of their active Units. Rest them.”. The wording admits more than one reading. The effect belongs to its controller, so for the opponent it is an enemy effect whoever does the choosing.
- Evidence:
  - card EB01-009: “All enemy players each choose 1 of their active Units. Rest them.”
- Sources: gcg-api card EB01-009 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=EB01-009 (effect)
- **Resolution** (`most_defensible_reading`): The opponent makes the choice (Q313). A Unit that 'can't be chosen by enemy effects' cannot be chosen.
  - Rationale: The effect belongs to its controller, so for the opponent it is an enemy effect whoever does the choosing.

### `ambiguous:EB01-017:player-who-destroyed-unit` — EB01-017 Haro: reading of “you and the player who destroyed this Unit draw 1”

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: EB01-017
- Card text: “you and the player who destroyed this Unit draw 1”. The wording admits more than one reading. In 1v1, battles are only between Units of opposing players.
- Evidence:
  - card EB01-017: “you and the player who destroyed this Unit draw 1”
- Sources: gcg-api card EB01-017 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=EB01-017 (effect)
- **Resolution** (`most_defensible_reading`): With battle damage, the destroyer is always the opponent.
  - Rationale: In 1v1, battles are only between Units of opposing players.

### `ambiguous:EB01-023:all-players-each-look` — EB01-023 Le Cygne (EX): reading of “All players each look at the top card of their deck. If it is a card t…”

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: EB01-023
- Card text: “All players each look at the top card of their deck. If it is a card that is Lv.5 or higher, they may reveal it and add it to their hand. They return any remaining card to the top or bottom of their deck.”. The wording admits more than one reading. Ruling Q319: players perform it in order starting with the active player, treated as simultaneous.
- Evidence:
  - card EB01-023: “All players each look at the top card of their deck. If it is a card that is Lv.5 or higher, they may reveal it and add it to their hand. They return any remaining card to the top or bottom of their deck.”
- Sources: gcg-api card EB01-023 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=EB01-023 (effect)
- **Resolution** (`most_defensible_reading`): The active player does the whole sequence (look, optional add, return top/bottom), then the standby player does the same. Each player learns only their own top card.
  - Rationale: Ruling Q319: players perform it in order starting with the active player, treated as simultaneous.

### `ambiguous:EB01-028:once-per-turn-when` — EB01-028 Gundam Plutone: reading of “【Once per Turn】When another Unit attacks an enemy Unit, if this Unit i…”

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: EB01-028
- Card text: “【Once per Turn】When another Unit attacks an enemy Unit, if this Unit is rested, the attacking Unit gains <Breach 2> during this battle.”. The wording admits more than one reading. 'enemy Unit' is relative to Plutone's controller, so the attacker must be friendly. Checking at trigger time (rule 10-2-1) stops an attack made while Plutone is active from using up 【Once per Turn】.
- Evidence:
  - card EB01-028: “【Once per Turn】When another Unit attacks an enemy Unit, if this Unit is rested, the attacking Unit gains <Breach 2> during this battle.”
- Sources: gcg-api card EB01-028 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=EB01-028 (effect)
- **Resolution** (`most_defensible_reading`): Triggers only for a friendly Unit other than Plutone that attacks an enemy Unit (not the player). The rested condition is checked when it triggers.
  - Rationale: 'enemy Unit' is relative to Plutone's controller, so the attacker must be friendly. Checking at trigger time (rule 10-2-1) stops an attack made while Plutone is active from using up 【Once per Turn】.

### `ambiguous:EB01-036:during-turn-all-other` — EB01-036 Darilbalde: reading of “During your turn, all other (G Generation) Units that are Lv.3 get AP+…”

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: EB01-036
- Card text: “During your turn, all other (G Generation) Units that are Lv.3 get AP+1.”. The wording admits more than one reading. The text says neither 'your' nor 'friendly', and 'all Units' covers both sides elsewhere in the set (EB01-003 ruling Q311, EB01-029).
- Evidence:
  - card EB01-036: “During your turn, all other (G Generation) Units that are Lv.3 get AP+1.”
- Sources: gcg-api card EB01-036 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=EB01-036 (effect)
- **Resolution** (`most_defensible_reading`): Applies to other (G Generation) Lv.3 Units on both sides.

### `ambiguous:EB01-042:attack-units-are-lv` — EB01-042 Psycho Haro (EX): reading of “【Attack】Units that are Lv.7 or lower can't activate <Blocker> during t…”

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: EB01-042
- Card text: “【Attack】Units that are Lv.7 or lower can't activate <Blocker> during this battle.”. The wording admits more than one reading. During this Unit's attack only the defending player can block, so 'enemy Units' and 'all Units' are equivalent in 1v1.

  - card EB01-042: “【Attack】Units that are Lv.7 or lower can't activate <Blocker> during this battle.”

- **Resolution** (`most_defensible_reading`): Applies to enemy Units only.
  - Rationale: During this Unit's attack only the defending player can block, so 'enemy Units' and 'all Units' are equivalent in 1v1.

### `ambiguous:EB01-050:if-placed-card-lv` — EB01-050 Saikoro Gundam: reading of “If you placed a card that is Lv.3 or higher with this effect”

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: EB01-050
- Card text: “If you placed a card that is Lv.3 or higher with this effect”. The wording admits more than one reading. The text says 'a card', not 'a Unit card'.

  - card EB01-050: “If you placed a card that is Lv.3 or higher with this effect”
- Sources: gcg-api card EB01-050 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=EB01-050 (effect)
- **Resolution** (`most_defensible_reading`): Any card type with a printed Lv of 3 or more counts (Unit, Pilot, Command, Base).

### `ambiguous:EB01-070:if-opponent-s-turn` — EB01-070 Daryl Lorenz: reading of “①:If it is your opponent's turn, choose 1 Unit. It gets AP+1 during th…”

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: EB01-070
- Card text: “①:If it is your opponent's turn, choose 1 Unit. It gets AP+1 during this battle.”. The wording admits more than one reading. 8-6-1 ends 'during this battle' effects when the battle ends; with no battle there is no period for the effect to apply (the same reading is used for every 'during this battle' effect, see resolution ambiguous:during-this-battle-without-battle).
- Evidence:
  - card EB01-070: “①:If it is your opponent's turn, choose 1 Unit. It gets AP+1 during this battle.”
- Sources: gcg-api card EB01-070 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=EB01-070 (effect)
- **Resolution** (`most_defensible_reading`): Activated with no battle in progress (e.g. in the end-phase action step), the 'during this battle' effect has no effect.
  - Rationale: 8-6-1 ends 'during this battle' effects when the battle ends; with no battle there is no period for the effect to apply (the same reading is used for every 'during this battle' effect, see resolution ambiguous:during-this-battle-without-battle).

### `ambiguous:EB01-074:if-do-all-enemy` — EB01-074 Eternal Road: reading of “If you do, all enemy players each choose 1 of their active Units. Rest…”

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: EB01-074
- Card text: “If you do, all enemy players each choose 1 of their active Units. Rest them.”. The wording admits more than one reading. The effect is controlled by the Command's player, so it is an enemy effect for the opponent's Units.
- Evidence:
  - card EB01-074: “If you do, all enemy players each choose 1 of their active Units. Rest them.”
- Sources: gcg-api card EB01-074 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=EB01-074 (effect)
- **Resolution** (`most_defensible_reading`): The opponent chooses one of their own active Units (Q329). That choice counts as targeting by the Command's controller, so Units that 'can't be chosen by enemy effects' are excluded.
  - Rationale: The effect is controlled by the Command's player, so it is an enemy effect for the opponent's Units.

### `ambiguous:EB01-077:choose-rested-friendly-g` — EB01-077 Master League Begins: reading of “Choose 1 rested friendly (G Generation) Unit. Change a battling enemy …”

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: EB01-077
- Card text: “Choose 1 rested friendly (G Generation) Unit. Change a battling enemy Unit's attack target to it.”. The wording admits more than one reading. An enemy Unit that is being attacked has no attack target; changing your own attacker's target would contradict 'enemy'.
- Evidence:
  - card EB01-077: “Choose 1 rested friendly (G Generation) Unit. Change a battling enemy Unit's attack target to it.”
- Sources: gcg-api card EB01-077 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=EB01-077 (effect)
- **Resolution** (`most_defensible_reading`): Only an enemy Unit that is attacking has an attack target, so the change happens only while an enemy Unit is attacking. During your own attack nothing changes. The Command stays playable because the only chosen target is the rested friendly Unit (10-1-8-1-1).
  - Rationale: An enemy Unit that is being attacked has no attack target; changing your own attacker's target would contradict 'enemy'.

### `ambiguous:EB01-085:then-may-choose-active` — EB01-085 Kudelia Aina Bernstein & Isaribi: reading of “Then, you may choose 1 active friendly blue (G Generation) Unit and 1 …”

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: EB01-085
- Card text: “Then, you may choose 1 active friendly blue (G Generation) Unit and 1 enemy Unit. Rest them.”. The wording admits more than one reading. The CONFLICTS resolution for ruling:EB01-072:Q327 applies the same both-targets reading to identical 'choose 1 X and 1 Y' wording.
- Evidence:
  - card EB01-085: “Then, you may choose 1 active friendly blue (G Generation) Unit and 1 enemy Unit. Rest them.”
- Sources: gcg-api card EB01-085 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=EB01-085 (effect)
- **Resolution** (`most_defensible_reading`): The optional rest is offered only when both Units can be chosen. The enemy Unit may already be rested.
  - Rationale: The CONFLICTS resolution for ruling:EB01-072:Q327 applies the same both-targets reading to identical 'choose 1 X and 1 Y' wording.

### `ambiguous:GD01-003:if-do-set-unit` — GD01-003 Unicorn Gundam 02 Banshee (Destroy Mode): reading of “If you do, set this Unit as active. It gains <First Strike> during thi…”

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: GD01-003
- Card text: “If you do, set this Unit as active. It gains <First Strike> during this turn.”. The wording admits more than one reading. Cards returned to the deck can't gain keywords, so this Unit is the only sensible antecedent. The follow-on sentence continues the 'If you do' result. In practice it is moot, because under Q121 the effect only resolves when all 12 cards are returned.
- Evidence:
  - card GD01-003: “If you do, set this Unit as active. It gains <First Strike> during this turn.”
- Sources: gcg-api card GD01-003 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-003 (effect)
- **Resolution** (`most_defensible_reading`): 'It' is this Unit, and both the set-active and the First Strike grant depend on 'If you do'
  - Rationale: Cards returned to the deck can't gain keywords, so this Unit is the only sensible antecedent. The follow-on sentence continues the 'If you do' result. In practice it is moot, because under Q121 the effect only resolves when all 12 cards are returned.

### `ambiguous:GD01-058:choose-unit-lv-4` — GD01-058 Galluss-K: reading of “Choose 1 Unit that is Lv.4 or higher. It gets AP+1 during this battle.”

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: GD01-058
- Card text: “Choose 1 Unit that is Lv.4 or higher. It gets AP+1 during this battle.”. The wording admits more than one reading. 8-6-1 ends 'during this battle' effects when the battle ends; with no battle there is no period for the effect to apply (the same reading is used for every 'during this battle' effect, see resolution ambiguous:during-this-battle-without-battle).
- Evidence:
  - card GD01-058: “Choose 1 Unit that is Lv.4 or higher. It gets AP+1 during this battle.”
- Sources: gcg-api card GD01-058 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-058 (effect)
- **Resolution** (`most_defensible_reading`): Activated with no battle in progress (e.g. in the end-phase action step), the 'during this battle' effect has no effect.
  - Rationale: 8-6-1 ends 'during this battle' effects when the battle ends; with no battle there is no period for the effect to apply (the same reading is used for every 'during this battle' effect, see resolution ambiguous:during-this-battle-without-battle).

### `ambiguous:GD01-063:during-turn-while-unit` — GD01-063 ZnO: reading of “During your turn, while this Unit is battling an enemy Unit that is Lv…”

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: GD01-063
- Card text: “During your turn, while this Unit is battling an enemy Unit that is Lv.2 or lower, it gains <First Strike>.”. The wording admits more than one reading. Matches ruling Q136 (a block by a Lv4 Unit removes First Strike). Tokens count as Lv0 (5-17-2-4), so they qualify.
- Evidence:
  - card GD01-063: “During your turn, while this Unit is battling an enemy Unit that is Lv.2 or lower, it gains <First Strike>.”
- Sources: gcg-api card GD01-063 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-063 (effect)
- **Resolution** (`most_defensible_reading`): 'Battling' is the current attacker/target pair of the ongoing battle (5-22-1, 5-22-2). A block changes the battling Unit, and the grant is re-evaluated continuously.
  - Rationale: Matches ruling Q136 (a block by a Lv4 Unit removes First Strike). Tokens count as Lv0 (5-17-2-4), so they qualify.

### `ambiguous:GD01-066:choose-triple-ship-alliance` — GD01-066 Justice Gundam: reading of “Choose 1 of your (Triple Ship Alliance) Unit tokens. It may attack on …”

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: GD01-066
- Card text: “Choose 1 of your (Triple Ship Alliance) Unit tokens. It may attack on the turn it is deployed.”. The wording admits more than one reading. The permission can only matter on the token's deploy turn, so 'this turn' and 'while in play' give the same result.
- Evidence:
  - card GD01-066: “Choose 1 of your (Triple Ship Alliance) Unit tokens. It may attack on the turn it is deployed.”
- Sources: gcg-api card GD01-066 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-066 (effect)
- **Resolution** (`most_defensible_reading`): The permission to attack on the turn it is deployed lasts this turn only.
  - Rationale: The permission can only matter on the token's deploy turn, so 'this turn' and 'while in play' give the same result.

- Evidence:
  - card GD01-082: “It gets AP-1 during this battle.”

### `ambiguous:GD01-091:during-turn-if-unit` — GD01-091 Chang Wufei: reading of “During your turn, if this Unit has <Breach>, it can't receive battle d…”

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: GD01-091
- Card text: “During your turn, if this Unit has <Breach>, it can't receive battle damage from enemy Units with 3 or less AP.”. The wording admits more than one reading. The prevention is a constant effect checked at the moment of damage (10-1-5-3).
- Evidence:
  - card GD01-091: “During your turn, if this Unit has <Breach>, it can't receive battle damage from enemy Units with 3 or less AP.”
- Sources: gcg-api card GD01-091 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-091 (effect)
- **Resolution** (`most_defensible_reading`): The source's AP is measured when the damage is dealt, and the source must be an enemy Unit.
  - Rationale: The prevention is a constant effect checked at the moment of damage (10-1-5-3).

### `ambiguous:GD01-094:once-per-turn-when` — GD01-094 Yzak Jule: reading of “【Once per Turn】 When an enemy Link Unit is destroyed with damage while…”

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: GD01-094
- Card text: “【Once per Turn】 When an enemy Link Unit is destroyed with damage while this Unit is attacking, draw 1.”. The wording admits more than one reading. Q149, FAQ Q98, and the GD02-093:Q190 resolution for simultaneous destruction (8-5-3-2-3, 10-1-6-4).
- Evidence:
  - card GD01-094: “【Once per Turn】 When an enemy Link Unit is destroyed with damage while this Unit is attacking, draw 1.”
- Sources: gcg-api card GD01-094 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-094 (effect)
- **Resolution** (`most_defensible_reading`): 'With damage' means battle damage or effect damage that brings the Unit to 0 HP; a 'destroy' effect does not count (Q149). 'Link Unit' uses the linked status at destruction (last known information). It also triggers when this Unit is destroyed in the same damage step, since at that moment it was attacking.
  - Rationale: Q149, FAQ Q98, and the GD02-093:Q190 resolution for simultaneous destruction (8-5-3-2-3, 10-1-6-4).

### `ambiguous:GD01-097:activate-main-once-per` — GD01-097 Guel Jeturk: reading of “【Activate･Main】【Once per Turn】If your opponent has 8 or more cards in …”

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: GD01-097
- Card text: “【Activate･Main】【Once per Turn】If your opponent has 8 or more cards in their hand, set this Unit as active. It can't attack during this turn.”. The wording admits more than one reading. 'It' is the Unit set active by the conditional sentence. With the condition false the activation performs nothing, which under resolution ruling:GD02-002:Q197 does not use 【Once per Turn】, so not offering it changes no reachable outcome. Q150: the restriction outlasts the hand shrinking.
- Evidence:
  - card GD01-097: “【Activate･Main】【Once per Turn】If your opponent has 8 or more cards in their hand, set this Unit as active. It can't attack during this turn.”
- Sources: gcg-api card GD01-097 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-097 (effect)
- **Resolution** (`most_defensible_reading`): The can't-attack restriction applies only when the If holds. Activation is offered only while the opponent has 8 or more cards.
  - Rationale: 'It' is the Unit set active by the conditional sentence. With the condition false the activation performs nothing, which under resolution ruling:GD02-002:Q197 does not use 【Once per Turn】, so not offering it changes no reachable outcome. Q150: the restriction outlasts the hand shrinking.

### `ambiguous:GD01-098:activate-action-once-per` — GD01-098 Elan Ceres (Enhanced Person Number 4): reading of “【Activate･Action】【Once per Turn】If an enemy Unit with 1 or less AP is …”

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
- Cards: GD01-098
- Card text: “【Activate･Action】【Once per Turn】If an enemy Unit with 1 or less AP is in play, this Unit recovers 1 HP.”. The wording admits more than one reading. Otherwise the activation performs nothing (recovering an undamaged Unit does nothing), which under resolution ruling:GD02-002:Q197 does not use 【Once per Turn】.
- Evidence:
  - card GD01-098: “【Activate･Action】【Once per Turn】If an enemy Unit with 1 or less AP is in play, this Unit recovers 1 HP.”
- Sources: gcg-api card GD01-098 — https://www.gundam-gcg.com/en/cards/detail.php?detailSearch=GD01-098 (effect)
- **Resolution** (`most_defensible_reading`): Offered only when an enemy Unit with AP<=1 exists and this Unit is damaged.
  - Rationale: Otherwise the activation performs nothing (recovering an undamaged Unit does nothing), which under resolution ruling:GD02-002:Q197 does not use 【Once per Turn】.

### `ambiguous:GD01-110:main-action-choose-unit` — GD01-110 Rasid's Orders: reading of “【Main】/【Action】Choose 1 Unit that is Lv.4 or higher. During this turn,…”

- severity **minor**; origin curated; changes engine behaviour
